esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix ↗ 4 releases · 4 stable
v2.40.0Reasonix CLI v2.40.0 Stable2026-10-14T14:02:13Z

CLI archives built from the Studio release studio-v2.40.0.

This release fixes the unclickable workspace "+" button, adds a turn-narration setting, and lets the changelog switch between Chinese and English.

New

  • The feedback dialog gains "Capture this window": it takes one picture of the app window only when you click, adds it as a removable thumbnail, and never sends it on its own. Refs #11965.
  • The session tree now carries each conversation's last-activity time, so a list can sort by recency or show a relative time. Refs #12011.
  • A sub-agent queued behind another's write claim now says whose claim it waits on, and whether neither declared write_paths (each treated as writing the whole workspace). Refs #12052.
  • Theme packs gain shadowFloat, the shadow menus, popovers and dialogs cast; unset keeps the built-in one. Refs #12063.
  • Charts now write each value beside its bar or line point while the plot is sparse, and draw none when there are too many marks to read; hover readout is unchanged. Refs #12291.
  • Projects in the sidebar can now be reordered by dragging a row; a line on the target's top or bottom edge shows where it lands, and the order is kept by the kernel. Refs #12300.
  • The "Worked for" line at the end of a turn now also says when the turn began (time of day for today, with the date for another day), and it survives reopening the session. Refs #12728.
  • Settings > Account gains "Export and import config": no account needed; export an encrypted file and import it item by item elsewhere. Keys off by default; a passphrase is required. Refs #12736.
  • Settings → Session gains "Narration between tool calls": Detailed asks the model for three or four lines between batches of tool calls; Brief, the default, changes nothing. Refs #12788.
  • The project menu gains "Open with …": besides the file manager, open a project folder in the VS Code, Cursor, IntelliJ IDEA or system terminal this machine has installed. Refs #12858.
  • The workbench explorer gains a "Commit history" section: opened, it lists the newest commits of every branch as a graph, with branches and tags. Refs #12913.
  • Settings → Appearance gains a "Sessions from 1.x" switch to hide migrated 1.x sessions from the sidebar; shown by default, nothing is deleted. Refs #12964.
  • In the terminal UI, /theme now opens a picker that previews each row as you move, keeps it on Enter and restores the original on Esc; /theme list still prints the names. Refs #12985.
  • When the context is over the compaction threshold with nothing to fold, a notice now says what holds the tokens, by class and largest items. Refs #13013.
  • Local stdio MCP servers now receive the extra folders you allowed tools to write in _meta["io.reasonix/writableRoots"]; remote ones do not. Refs #13053.
  • First-run setup gains "Not now": open the workbench first, with a note above the input box leading back to setup; this launch only, offered again next time. Refs #13065.
  • The bottom "Generation speed" detail gains "Time to first token" (request sent to first output, timed by the kernel) and now uses the same row layout as the context detail. Refs #13075.
  • Adding a project now selects it and opens a new session in it (nothing is sent for you), whether through "Open or create project", the first-run prompt or a folder drop. Refs #13076.
  • New floating "Back to top" / "Jump to latest" buttons in the conversation (hidden near an end or when nothing scrolls) land on the nearest expanded block first. Refs #13077.
  • The Versions page can switch a release's notes between Chinese and English, following the interface language by default; every release's notes now carry English. Refs #13081.

Changed

  • The Files button in the rail and sidebar now toggles: pressed while the files panel is open it folds it, and it reopens after the panel was closed elsewhere. Refs #12925.
  • The composer's model menu now sets provider headings apart, indents the models, and adds multi-select provider chips that are remembered. Refs #12926.
  • Restore the black-and-gold application, taskbar/Dock, launcher and tray icons. Refs #13004.
  • Sent pictures, pictures waiting in the composer and tool screenshots share one viewer: open the original, Esc to leave, focus returns. Refs #13006, #13010.
  • The Settings sidebar's selected row no longer carries the accent colour, outline and edge bar, only a deeper fill; section titles are lighter than the items under them. Refs #13045, #13046.
  • The title-bar Browser button is gone: the Workbench tab is the one way in, and with nothing running it shows the Files / Changes / Browser start page instead of opening a blank page. Refs #13067.
  • Session rows no longer show the hover Archive and Delete icons; both stay in the right-click menu, and the Delete key still works. Refs #13068.

Fixed

  • When the model cannot draft a commit message, the commit card still lists the staged files and says why; type the message and commit. Refs #12747.
  • The context gauge now reads the provider's reported size plus what grew since, instead of the larger of that and the local estimate, so an estimate running high no longer inflates it. Refs #12988.
  • A read-only find ... -exec wc/grep/cat/stat ... is no longer treated as a write, so it does not claim the workspace write lease or collide with another session's edit. Refs #12993.
  • The repeated-output guard now also flags a stream that collapses to a few tiny lines in no fixed order; before it only saw byte-identical loops. Refs #13001.
  • Each sub-agent in the sub-agents popover now shows its own task title (the first line of its description, cut to fit, full text on hover), so parallel ones no longer all read "task". Refs #13002.
  • Switching to a long session no longer shows "could not restore extension surfaces"; you hear once only if a surface was dropped. Refs #13011.
  • On the run-analysis signal rail, marks too close together now share one counted marker that opens a list, instead of stacking into a tall column. Refs #13034.
  • Fixed the project row's "…" button answering only at its corners: the faded count badge kept sitting over it and taking the click; it no longer takes the pointer. Refs #13036, #13038.
  • The magnifier beside the session filters is now a real toggle: pressing it again closes the find box instead of replaying its opening animation. Refs #13041.
  • The "+" that opens or creates a project folder on the sidebar's "This machine" row is now always visible instead of appearing only when the row is hovered. Refs #13043, #13072.
  • Fixed the branch hover card staying on screen under the branch menu and stacking a second shadow. Refs #13044.
  • The red notice raised when reading the session status fails on reload now clears itself when that read next succeeds, instead of waiting for "Got it". Refs #13047.
  • Failures that do not block sending now show as corner notices (three at most, dismissed only by you) with "Report this problem". Refs #13047, #13048.
  • The extension-surface, MCP catalog and changed-files reads treat a missing list as empty instead of raising "Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'filter')". Refs #13055.
  • The branch list, event replay and queue now answer an empty list instead of null when they are empty, so the page no longer fails reading them. Refs #13055.
  • The "Report this problem" entry on error bars and corner notices now also writes a page defect's error name and where it came from (file name and line only) into the feedback draft. Refs #13055.
  • A red notice under the composer that comes from a defect of the page itself (for example "Cannot read properties of undefined") now also logs its stack to the console. Refs #13055.
  • Uploading a large image or file from the phone remote page no longer drops the connection: an oversized request is refused on the phone with a clear message and the link keeps working. Refs #13056.
  • The phone remote page can now upload larger images and files, sent as several messages and handled once the desktop has them all (up to 16 MB). Refs #13056.
  • Fixed the repeated "Checkpoint recording failed" warning for writes outside the workspace, such as the session temp folder: they are now a coverage gap, not a failure. Refs #13060.
  • Fixed the kernel crashing when an MCP server fails to launch or reconnect: a failed server now only becomes unavailable, nothing else is affected. Refs #13086.
  • Fixed one likely cause of broken images (such as the sidebar wordmark) on some Windows machines: the page's file types no longer follow other programs' registry entries. Refs #13094.

View release →

studio-v2.40.0Reasonix Studio v2.40.0 Stable2026-10-14T13:59:35Z

本版本修复工作区「+」无法点击等问题，新增回合叙述设置，并让更新日志支持中英文切换。

新增

  • 反馈对话框新增「截取当前窗口」：只在你点按钮时截一次，截图以可删除的缩略图加入附件，不会自动发送。关联 #11965 fixed in #13033。
  • 侧边栏的会话列表数据现在带「最后活动时间」，可按最近使用排序或显示相对时间。关联 #12011 fixed in #12995。
  • 子代理因另一个子代理占着要写的路径而排队时，现在会说明在等谁、是否因为双方都没声明 write_paths（按整个工作区处理）。关联 #12052。
  • 主题包新增 shadowFloat，可改菜单、弹出层和对话框的投影；不设则保持内置阴影。关联 #12063 fixed in #12996。
  • 图表在数据不多时，会在柱顶和折线数据点旁直接标出数值；数据多到放不下时自动不画，悬停读数仍然可用。关联 #12291。
  • 侧栏里的项目现在可以拖动排序：按住项目行拖到目标位置，上沿或下沿的线指示落点；顺序保存在内核里。关联 #12300。
  • 每轮结束时的「耗时」行现在同时写出这一轮的开始时间（当天只显示时分，其他日子带日期），重新载入会话后仍然保留。关联 #12728 fixed in #12994。
  • 设置 > 账号新增「导出与导入配置」：无需账号，加密导出成文件、换机器时逐项导入；密钥默认不导出，必须设口令。关联 #12736。
  • 设置 → 会话新增「回合间叙述」：选「详细」后，模型会在每批工具调用之间用三四行说明拿到了什么、下一步做什么；默认「简短」，行为不变。关联 #12788 fixed in #13101。
  • 项目右键菜单新增「用 … 打开」：文件管理器之外，可用本机已安装的 VS Code、Cursor、IntelliJ IDEA、系统终端打开项目文件夹。关联 #12858。
  • 工作台文件面板里新增「提交历史」：展开后按提交图谱列出所有分支最近的提交，带分支和标签。关联 #12913 fixed in #13028。
  • 设置 → 外观新增「1.x 旧会话」开关，可把迁移来的 1.x 会话从侧栏隐藏；默认显示，不删除，搜索和已打开的不受影响。关联 #12964 fixed in #13097。
  • 终端界面的 /theme 现在打开选择器：光标移到哪一行就即时预览，Enter 保留，Esc 还原；/theme list 仍列出名称。关联 #12985 fixed in #12999。
  • 上下文超过压缩阈值而无内容可折叠时，现在会说明由什么占用（按类别和最大几项），不再只说「无需压缩」。关联 #13013。
  • 本机 stdio MCP 服务器现在会在 _meta["io.reasonix/writableRoots"] 里收到你额外允许写入的目录；远程服务器不会收到。关联 #13053。
  • 首次启动的设置页新增「稍后再说」：可先进入主界面，输入框上方会提示「去连接」；只对本次启动有效，下次仍会提示。关联 #13065 fixed in #13084。
  • 底部「生成速度」详情新增「首字时间」（从发出请求到首个输出，由内核计时），并改为与「上下文详情」一致的行式样式。关联 #13075 fixed in #13089。
  • 添加项目后会自动选中该项目并打开一个新会话（不会替你发送任何消息），无论是点「打开或新建项目」、首次提示里的按钮还是拖入文件夹。关联 #13076 fixed in #13088。
  • 对话区右下角新增「回到顶部 / 跳到最新」悬浮按钮（距端不足 48px 或无需滚动时隐藏），优先跳到最近的展开块，取代原「回到最新」。关联 #13077 fixed in #13091。
  • 版本页的更新内容可以在中文和英文之间切换，默认跟随界面语言；之后每个版本的发布说明都附带英文。关联 #13081。

变更

  • 左侧图标栏和侧栏的「文件」按钮现在是开关：文件面板已展开时再点一次就收起，在别处关掉后再点会重新展开。关联 #12925 fixed in #12997。
  • 撰写框的模型菜单里，服务商标题更醒目、模型行缩进，并新增按服务商的筛选标签（可多选、可清除，选择会记住）。关联 #12926。
  • 应用、任务栏/Dock、启动器和托盘图标恢复为黑金配色。关联 #13004 by @SivanCola。
  • 对话里的图片、输入框里待发的图片和工具截图共用一个查看器：点击放大原图，Esc 退出，焦点回到缩略图。关联 #13006 fixed in #13022、#13010 fixed in #13022。
  • 设置侧栏的选中项不再带强调色、边框和左侧竖条，只是底色变深；分区标题比选项更轻，层级更清楚。关联 #13045 fixed in #13050、#13046 fixed in #13050。
  • 去掉顶栏的「浏览器」按钮：「工作台」标签是唯一入口，没有运行中的网页时显示「文件 / 改动 / 浏览器」启动页，不再直接开一个空白页。关联 #13067 fixed in #13095。
  • 会话行不再在悬停时显示「归档」「删除」两个小图标，界面更简洁；这两个操作仍在右键菜单里，Delete 键也照常可用。关联 #13068 fixed in #13079。

修复

  • 模型起草提交说明失败时，提交卡片仍列出暂存的文件，输入框留空并说明原因，可以手动填写后提交。关联 #12747 fixed in #12923 and #13005。
  • 上下文用量改为以服务商回报的大小加上之后新增的内容为准，不再取本地估算和回报中较大的一个，估算偏大时不再虚高。关联 #12988 fixed in #12991 and #13018。
  • 只读的 find ... -exec wc/grep/cat/stat ... 不再被当成写入：它不会占用工作区写入租约，也就不会再和另一个会话正在写的文件冲突。关联 #12993 fixed in #13003。
  • 模型退化成「很多句不重复的短句」（如 go. / End. / I wait.）时，重复输出检测现在也会提示；以前只认字节完全相同的循环。关联 #13001 fixed in #13008。
  • 子代理浮层里每个子代理现在显示自己的任务标题（来自任务描述的首行，过长截断，悬停看全文），并行的子代理不再都只写「task」。关联 #13002 fixed in #13014。
  • 切换长会话时不再弹出「扩展界面恢复失败」；只有确实丢了扩展界面才提示一次，读不出历史才报错。关联 #13011 fixed in #13019。
  • 运行分析的信号轨里挨得太近的标记合并成一个带数量的标记，点开列出明细，不再排成很长的竖列。关联 #13034 fixed in #13040。
  • 修复项目行上“…”按钮只有四个角能点、点中间没有反应的问题：行内的数量徽标淡出后仍然盖在按钮上，现在不再拦截鼠标。关联 #13036 fixed in #13042、#13038。
  • 侧栏会话筛选旁的放大镜现在是真正的开关：再点一次会收起搜索框，不再重播弹出动画。关联 #13041 fixed in #13049。
  • 侧栏「这台机器」行上打开或新建项目文件夹的“＋”按钮现在始终可见，不再要把鼠标移到行上才出现。关联 #13043 fixed in #13074、#13072。
  • 修复输入框里打开分支菜单时，悬停提示卡没有收起、与菜单叠成两层阴影的问题。关联 #13044 fixed in #13054。
  • 重新载入会话时读取状态失败而弹出的红色提示，在之后同一读取成功时会自动消失，不用再手动点“知道了”。关联 #13047。
  • 设置没生效、链接打不开等不阻断发送的失败，改为右下角的小提示（最多 3 条，点“知道了”才消失），可一键“报告此问题”。关联 #13047、#13048。
  • 扩展界面、MCP 目录和改动文件三处读取遇到缺失的列表字段时按空列表处理，不再弹出「Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'filter')」。关联 #13055。
  • 分支列表、事件回放和待发队列在为空时现在返回空列表而不是 null，页面不再因此在读取时出错。关联 #13055。
  • 报错条与右下角提示的「报告此问题」现在会把页面自身错误的名称和出错位置（仅文件名与行列号）一并填进反馈草稿，便于定位。关联 #13055。
  • 输入框下的红色提示若是页面自身的程序错误（如“Cannot read properties of undefined”），现在会把调用栈写进控制台，便于定位。关联 #13055。
  • 手机远控页上传较大的图片或文件时，不再把连接弄断：过大的内容会在手机上直接提示并留在原处，其他操作照常。关联 #13056。
  • 手机远控页现在可以上传较大的图片和文件：内容拆成多条消息发送，电脑端收齐后一次处理（最大 16 MB）；旧版电脑端仍提示太大。关联 #13056。
  • 修复写入会话临时目录等工作区外位置时反复弹出“检查点记录失败”的提示：这类写入不在检查点范围内，改为记为覆盖缺口。关联 #13060 fixed in #13063。
  • 修复 MCP 服务器启动或重连失败时内核崩溃退出的问题：失败现在只会让这个服务器不可用，不会影响其他功能。关联 #13086 fixed in #13090。
  • 修复部分 Windows 电脑上界面图片（如左上角字标）显示为破损图片的一个可能原因：页面文件的类型不再受注册表里别的程序设置的影响。关联 #13094。

贡献者

感谢本版本的贡献者：@SivanCola

<!-- lang:en -->

This release fixes the unclickable workspace "+" button, adds a turn-narration setting, and lets the changelog switch between Chinese and English.

New

  • The feedback dialog gains "Capture this window": it takes one picture of the app window only when you click, adds it as a removable thumbnail, and never sends it on its own. Refs #11965 fixed in #13033.
  • The session tree now carries each conversation's last-activity time, so a list can sort by recency or show a relative time. Refs #12011 fixed in #12995.
  • A sub-agent queued behind another's write claim now says whose claim it waits on, and whether neither declared write_paths (each treated as writing the whole workspace). Refs #12052.
  • Theme packs gain shadowFloat, the shadow menus, popovers and dialogs cast; unset keeps the built-in one. Refs #12063 fixed in #12996.
  • Charts now write each value beside its bar or line point while the plot is sparse, and draw none when there are too many marks to read; hover readout is unchanged. Refs #12291.
  • Projects in the sidebar can now be reordered by dragging a row; a line on the target's top or bottom edge shows where it lands, and the order is kept by the kernel. Refs #12300.
  • The "Worked for" line at the end of a turn now also says when the turn began (time of day for today, with the date for another day), and it survives reopening the session. Refs #12728 fixed in #12994.
  • Settings > Account gains "Export and import config": no account needed; export an encrypted file and import it item by item elsewhere. Keys off by default; a passphrase is required. Refs #12736.
  • Settings → Session gains "Narration between tool calls": Detailed asks the model for three or four lines between batches of tool calls; Brief, the default, changes nothing. Refs #12788 fixed in #13101.
  • The project menu gains "Open with …": besides the file manager, open a project folder in the VS Code, Cursor, IntelliJ IDEA or system terminal this machine has installed. Refs #12858.
  • The workbench explorer gains a "Commit history" section: opened, it lists the newest commits of every branch as a graph, with branches and tags. Refs #12913 fixed in #13028.
  • Settings → Appearance gains a "Sessions from 1.x" switch to hide migrated 1.x sessions from the sidebar; shown by default, nothing is deleted. Refs #12964 fixed in #13097.
  • In the terminal UI, /theme now opens a picker that previews each row as you move, keeps it on Enter and restores the original on Esc; /theme list still prints the names. Refs #12985 fixed in #12999.
  • When the context is over the compaction threshold with nothing to fold, a notice now says what holds the tokens, by class and largest items. Refs #13013.
  • Local stdio MCP servers now receive the extra folders you allowed tools to write in _meta["io.reasonix/writableRoots"]; remote ones do not. Refs #13053.
  • First-run setup gains "Not now": open the workbench first, with a note above the input box leading back to setup; this launch only, offered again next time. Refs #13065 fixed in #13084.
  • The bottom "Generation speed" detail gains "Time to first token" (request sent to first output, timed by the kernel) and now uses the same row layout as the context detail. Refs #13075 fixed in #13089.
  • Adding a project now selects it and opens a new session in it (nothing is sent for you), whether through "Open or create project", the first-run prompt or a folder drop. Refs #13076 fixed in #13088.
  • New floating "Back to top" / "Jump to latest" buttons in the conversation (hidden near an end or when nothing scrolls) land on the nearest expanded block first. Refs #13077 fixed in #13091.
  • The Versions page can switch a release's notes between Chinese and English, following the interface language by default; every release's notes now carry English. Refs #13081.

Changed

  • The Files button in the rail and sidebar now toggles: pressed while the files panel is open it folds it, and it reopens after the panel was closed elsewhere. Refs #12925 fixed in #12997.
  • The composer's model menu now sets provider headings apart, indents the models, and adds multi-select provider chips that are remembered. Refs #12926.
  • Restore the black-and-gold application, taskbar/Dock, launcher and tray icons. Refs #13004 by @SivanCola.
  • Sent pictures, pictures waiting in the composer and tool screenshots share one viewer: open the original, Esc to leave, focus returns. Refs #13006 fixed in #13022, #13010 fixed in #13022.
  • The Settings sidebar's selected row no longer carries the accent colour, outline and edge bar, only a deeper fill; section titles are lighter than the items under them. Refs #13045 fixed in #13050, #13046 fixed in #13050.
  • The title-bar Browser button is gone: the Workbench tab is the one way in, and with nothing running it shows the Files / Changes / Browser start page instead of opening a blank page. Refs #13067 fixed in #13095.
  • Session rows no longer show the hover Archive and Delete icons; both stay in the right-click menu, and the Delete key still works. Refs #13068 fixed in #13079.

Fixed

  • When the model cannot draft a commit message, the commit card still lists the staged files and says why; type the message and commit. Refs #12747 fixed in #12923 and #13005.
  • The context gauge now reads the provider's reported size plus what grew since, instead of the larger of that and the local estimate, so an estimate running high no longer inflates it. Refs #12988 fixed in #12991 and #13018.
  • A read-only find ... -exec wc/grep/cat/stat ... is no longer treated as a write, so it does not claim the workspace write lease or collide with another session's edit. Refs #12993 fixed in #13003.
  • The repeated-output guard now also flags a stream that collapses to a few tiny lines in no fixed order; before it only saw byte-identical loops. Refs #13001 fixed in #13008.
  • Each sub-agent in the sub-agents popover now shows its own task title (the first line of its description, cut to fit, full text on hover), so parallel ones no longer all read "task". Refs #13002 fixed in #13014.
  • Switching to a long session no longer shows "could not restore extension surfaces"; you hear once only if a surface was dropped. Refs #13011 fixed in #13019.
  • On the run-analysis signal rail, marks too close together now share one counted marker that opens a list, instead of stacking into a tall column. Refs #13034 fixed in #13040.
  • Fixed the project row's "…" button answering only at its corners: the faded count badge kept sitting over it and taking the click; it no longer takes the pointer. Refs #13036 fixed in #13042, #13038.
  • The magnifier beside the session filters is now a real toggle: pressing it again closes the find box instead of replaying its opening animation. Refs #13041 fixed in #13049.
  • The "+" that opens or creates a project folder on the sidebar's "This machine" row is now always visible instead of appearing only when the row is hovered. Refs #13043 fixed in #13074, #13072.
  • Fixed the branch hover card staying on screen under the branch menu and stacking a second shadow. Refs #13044 fixed in #13054.
  • The red notice raised when reading the session status fails on reload now clears itself when that read next succeeds, instead of waiting for "Got it". Refs #13047.
  • Failures that do not block sending now show as corner notices (three at most, dismissed only by you) with "Report this problem". Refs #13047, #13048.
  • The extension-surface, MCP catalog and changed-files reads treat a missing list as empty instead of raising "Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'filter')". Refs #13055.
  • The branch list, event replay and queue now answer an empty list instead of null when they are empty, so the page no longer fails reading them. Refs #13055.
  • The "Report this problem" entry on error bars and corner notices now also writes a page defect's error name and where it came from (file name and line only) into the feedback draft. Refs #13055.
  • A red notice under the composer that comes from a defect of the page itself (for example "Cannot read properties of undefined") now also logs its stack to the console. Refs #13055.
  • Uploading a large image or file from the phone remote page no longer drops the connection: an oversized request is refused on the phone with a clear message and the link keeps working. Refs #13056.
  • The phone remote page can now upload larger images and files, sent as several messages and handled once the desktop has them all (up to 16 MB). Refs #13056.
  • Fixed the repeated "Checkpoint recording failed" warning for writes outside the workspace, such as the session temp folder: they are now a coverage gap, not a failure. Refs #13060 fixed in #13063.
  • Fixed the kernel crashing when an MCP server fails to launch or reconnect: a failed server now only becomes unavailable, nothing else is affected. Refs #13086 fixed in #13090.
  • Fixed one likely cause of broken images (such as the sidebar wordmark) on some Windows machines: the page's file types no longer follow other programs' registry entries. Refs #13094.

Thanks to this release's contributors: @SivanCola

macOS 已用 Developer ID 签名并公证，打开时不需要清除隔离属性。Windows 的窗口、内核与安装器都经 Certum 代码签名证书做了 Authenticode 签名。Linux Debian 系从 .deb 安装，Fedora、RHEL、openSUSE 等从 .rpm 安装（sudo dnf install ./ReasonixStudio-linux-amd64.rpm）。x86_64 用文件名带 amd64 的包，aarch64（统信 UOS、麒麟等）用带 arm64 的包。

三个平台都能自更新：Linux 的 .deb 走自己的包（.rpm 的自更新尚未提供，请手动下载新包），Windows 运行下一个安装器，macOS 替换自身 bundle。

v2.39.0Reasonix CLI v2.39.0 Stable2026-10-14T01:27:37Z

CLI archives built from the Studio release studio-v2.39.0.

Changes in 2.39.0

This release adds double-click rename for sessions, starting without any model service, a low-disk-space warning and maps and dashboards in extension surfaces, and tidies the sidebar and conversation display.

New

  • Double-click a session row in the sidebar to rename it in place; the rename dialog can generate a title on request (#12977, #11591 by @Guan0923)
  • Reasonix starts without any model service configured and opens the connection guide directly (#12966)
  • A warning appears when the disk holding a project or session is nearly full; on by default, it only warns and never deletes files (#12960)
  • The terminal UI reports working, waiting-for-you and finished states to the terminal (#12976)
  • A finished session can stay in the Running list for a chosen time; off by default (#12957)
  • The sidebar marks sessions stored by 1.x, and the terminal resume list marks sessions you have not viewed (#12405, #12587)
  • A skill invoked with / shows as a compact skill block, and the JSON input of tool calls is syntax-coloured (#12972, #12969)
  • Files can be staged and unstaged from the changes list (#12923)
  • The application icon shows the count of unread finished tasks, and a new flat icon replaces the old one (#12922, #12897)
  • New "Clear blue" and "Tea green" colour schemes; theme packs can declare density and corner radius (#12932, #12513)
  • The icon rail has a New session button; clicking the folder mark in the title bar opens the project folder (#12891, #12857, #12288 by @keeyangyy)
  • File links in a conversation open in the workbench, and the file menu can add a file or folder to the conversation (#12910, #12877)
  • Remote machines are gathered into one collapsible row, and the levels of the session tree are easier to tell apart (#12878, #12905)
  • Esc must be pressed a second time to stop a running turn (#12859)
  • Extension surfaces support style fields, editable drafts, map-versus-dashboard views and non-blocking actions (#12830, #12854, #12876, #12896)
  • Installed packages can be searched by capability, show their contribution types, copy their invocation, and the install preview shows the resolved commit (#12065, #12117, #12070, #12075, #12098)
  • You can view a sub-agent's skill declarations and search an MCP server's tool list (#12102, #12085)
  • WSL distributions are discovered and one of them is probed (#12595)
  • The phone page can be added to the home screen; paired phones survive a restart, with a switch in settings (#12591, #12440 by @keeyangyy)
  • Linux arm64 .deb and .rpm packages are published (#12914)
  • Local stdio MCP servers receive the working directory and the host's read-only state (#12967)
  • The tool catalogue refreshes automatically when an MCP server adds or removes tools at runtime and sends a notification (#12138 by @boscocp)
  • The host's check of changed turns can be turned off (#12860)
  • Right-to-left text blocks are displayed in their own direction (#12118 by @xxoingr)
  • The workbench browser-tab menu gains "Close browser tabs to the right", reachable by keyboard (#12557 by @SivanCola)

Changed

  • The Versions page keeps only the latest few earlier versions and folds the rest behind a button (#12982)
  • Session lists use single-line ellipsis everywhere, and bulk selection moved to the "This machine" more menu (#12935 by @SivanCola)
  • The desktop sidebar uses divider bars for its actions, and the brand mark is the official wordmark throughout (#12918, #12936)
  • Computer use now runs on the CUA Driver (#12937)
  • The "Execution" block in a conversation is a faint single line, and its header sticks to the top when expanded (#12954, #12944)
  • The session title in the title bar sits next to the project name, and the branch button's hover shape matches its neighbours (#12955, #12971)
  • The model list keeps only the settings page's own scrollbar, and the display-alias input follows the theme (#12979)
  • Installed skills are grouped by source (#12329 by @KHG420)

Fixed

  • Folded user messages no longer vanish entirely when compaction cannot get a summary (#12963)
  • The context-usage figure now uses the same number the compaction trigger uses, instead of reading lower than the provider reports (#12991, #12988)
  • On Windows, clicking a task-completed notification brings the window to the front, and the click path is logged (#12907, #12956)
  • Closing a browser tab no longer reads content the kernel never sends (#12886)
  • A question you have started answering no longer times out and is skipped (#12884 by @par73e)
  • A turn in progress is kept when the runtime is rebuilt or a turn-end event is lost (#11735 by @jf200s)
  • Saving settings now reports success, not applied, and failure separately, and a failure can be retried (#12350 by @KHG420)
  • The undo entry is restored from the backend, and an expired-undo state is kept (#11589 by @Guan0923)
  • Unsaved file drafts in the workbench are kept (#12898 by @XTLine)
  • Context usage refreshes immediately after switching model settings (#12871 by @xlyy1)
  • Cost is shown in the wallet's currency, and the folder picker can list hidden folders (#12874)
  • On Windows, when the Git Bash probe hangs it is retried once, and the fallback shell path is shown correctly (#12887)
  • When only /skill-name is sent, history records exactly what you typed (#12890)
  • Duplicate MCP tool names are refused, and the original identifier stays the same on every path (#12889, #12893, #12892)
  • Sub-agent status frames are no longer lost when the client falls behind (#12888)
  • File preview checks that the target may be accessed, and passes through the PreToolUse hook first (#12115 by @Guan0923)
  • A failed memory save is rolled back, and a migrated memory repairs its index when changed again (#12938, #12939, #12940)

The Studio release page lists the same changes in Chinese and English: https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/releases/tag/studio-v2.39.0

studio-v2.39.0Reasonix Studio v2.39.0 Stable2026-10-14T01:24:47Z

本版加入会话行双击重命名、没有模型服务也能启动、磁盘空间不足提醒和扩展界面的地图与仪表盘，并整理侧边栏与对话的显示细节。

新增

  • 双击侧边栏会话行就地重命名；重命名对话框可以按需自动起标题 (#12977, #11591 by @Guan0923)
  • 没有配置任何模型服务也能启动，直接打开连接引导 (#12966)
  • 项目或会话所在磁盘快满时发出提醒，默认开启，只提醒不清理文件 (#12960)
  • 终端界面向终端报告工作中、等待你、已完成的状态 (#12976)
  • 已完成的会话可以在「进行中」列表里多留一段时间，默认关闭 (#12957)
  • 侧边栏标出保存在 1.x 里的会话，终端恢复列表标出未查看的会话 (#12405 by @keeyangyy, #12587)
  • 用 / 调用技能时显示为紧凑的技能块；工具调用的 JSON 输入带语法着色 (#12972, #12969)
  • 变更列表里可以暂存和取消暂存文件 (#12923)
  • 应用图标显示未读的任务完成数，并换成扁平的新图标 (#12922 by @KHG420, #12897)
  • 新增「清白蓝」「茶绿」配色，主题包可以声明密度和圆角 (#12932 by @SivanCola, #12513 by @f1shsmell)
  • 图标栏有新建会话按钮；点标题栏的文件夹标记可以打开项目文件夹 (#12891, #12857, #12288 by @keeyangyy)
  • 对话里的文件链接可以在工作台打开；文件菜单可以把文件或文件夹加入对话 (#12910 by @KHG420, #12877)
  • 远程机器收进一个可折叠的行，会话树各层级更容易分清 (#12878, #12905)
  • 第二次按 Esc 才会停止正在运行的回合 (#12859)
  • 扩展界面支持样式字段、可编辑草稿、地图对比仪表盘和非阻塞动作 (#12830, #12854, #12876, #12896)
  • 已安装的包可以按功能搜索、显示贡献类型、复制调用方式，安装预览显示解析出的提交 (#12065 by @KHG420, #12117 by @KHG420, #12070 by @KHG420, #12075 by @KHG420, #12098 by @KHG420)
  • 可以查看子代理的技能声明，并搜索 MCP 服务器的工具清单 (#12102 by @KHG420, #12085 by @KHG420)
  • 发现 WSL 发行版并探测其中一个 (#12595)
  • 手机页可以添加到主屏幕；已配对的手机在重启后保留，设置里有开关 (#12591, #12440 by @keeyangyy)
  • 发布 Linux arm64 的 .deb 和 .rpm 安装包 (#12914)
  • 本机 stdio 的 MCP 服务器能收到工作目录和宿主的只读状态 (#12967)
  • MCP 服务器在运行中增删工具并发出通知时，工具目录会自动刷新 (#12138 by @boscocp)
  • 可以关闭宿主对已改动回合的检查 (#12860)
  • 从右到左的文字块按自身方向显示 (#12118 by @xxoingr)
  • 工作台浏览器标签菜单新增「关闭右侧浏览器标签」，可用键盘打开 (#12557 by @SivanCola)

变更

  • 版本页只保留最新的几个早期版本，其余折叠到一个按钮后 (#12982)
  • 会话列表统一为单行省略，批量选择入口移到「这台机器」的更多菜单 (#12935 by @SivanCola)
  • 桌面侧栏统一用分隔条操作；品牌标识统一为正式字标 (#12918 by @SivanCola, #12936 by @SivanCola)
  • 计算机操作改用 CUA Driver (#12937)
  • 对话里的「执行过程」改成淡色一行，展开后的头部会吸在顶部 (#12954, #12944)
  • 标题栏的会话标题紧挨项目名；分支按钮悬停的形状与相邻按钮一致 (#12955, #12971)
  • 模型列表只留设置页的一条滚动条，显示别名输入框跟随主题 (#12979)
  • 已安装的技能按来源分组 (#12329 by @KHG420)

修复

  • 压缩拿不到摘要时，被折叠的用户消息不再整段消失 (#12963)
  • 上下文占用的显示改为和触发压缩时用的数字一致，不再比服务商回报的偏小 (#12991, #12988 fixed in #12991)
  • Windows 上点任务完成通知会把窗口带到前台，点击过程写入日志 (#12907, #12956)
  • 关闭浏览器标签时不再读取内核不会发送的内容 (#12886)
  • 你已经开始作答的提问不再超时跳过 (#12884 by @par73e)
  • 运行时重建或回合结束事件丢失后，仍保留正在进行的回合 (#11735 by @jf200s)
  • 保存设置的结果区分成功、未应用和失败，失败后可以重试 (#12350 by @KHG420)
  • 撤销入口从后端恢复，撤销失效状态会保存下来 (#11589 by @Guan0923)
  • 工作台里未保存的文件草稿会被保留 (#12898 by @XTLine)
  • 切换模型设置后上下文占用立即刷新 (#12871 by @xlyy1)
  • 费用按钱包的币种显示，文件夹选择器能列出隐藏文件夹 (#12874)
  • Windows 上 Git Bash 探测挂起时重试一次，并正确显示回退用的终端路径 (#12887)
  • 只发 /技能名 时，历史里记录的是你输入的原文 (#12890)
  • MCP 工具重名会被拒绝，原始标识在各条路径里保持一致 (#12889 by @XTLine, #12893 by @XTLine, #12892 by @XTLine)
  • 子代理状态帧在客户端落后时不再丢失 (#12888)
  • 文件预览会先检查目标是否允许访问，并先经过 PreToolUse 钩子 (#12115 by @Guan0923)
  • 记忆保存失败时会撤回，迁移过的记忆再次修改时会修复索引 (#12938 by @chuiwenwei, #12939 by @chuiwenwei, #12940 by @chuiwenwei)

English

This release adds double-click rename for sessions, starting without any model service, a low-disk-space warning and maps and dashboards in extension surfaces, and tidies the sidebar and conversation display.

New

  • Double-click a session row in the sidebar to rename it in place; the rename dialog can generate a title on request (#12977, #11591 by @Guan0923)
  • Reasonix starts without any model service configured and opens the connection guide directly (#12966)
  • A warning appears when the disk holding a project or session is nearly full; on by default, it only warns and never deletes files (#12960)
  • The terminal UI reports working, waiting-for-you and finished states to the terminal (#12976)
  • A finished session can stay in the Running list for a chosen time; off by default (#12957)
  • The sidebar marks sessions stored by 1.x, and the terminal resume list marks sessions you have not viewed (#12405, #12587)
  • A skill invoked with / shows as a compact skill block, and the JSON input of tool calls is syntax-coloured (#12972, #12969)
  • Files can be staged and unstaged from the changes list (#12923)
  • The application icon shows the count of unread finished tasks, and a new flat icon replaces the old one (#12922, #12897)
  • New "Clear blue" and "Tea green" colour schemes; theme packs can declare density and corner radius (#12932, #12513)
  • The icon rail has a New session button; clicking the folder mark in the title bar opens the project folder (#12891, #12857, #12288 by @keeyangyy)
  • File links in a conversation open in the workbench, and the file menu can add a file or folder to the conversation (#12910, #12877)
  • Remote machines are gathered into one collapsible row, and the levels of the session tree are easier to tell apart (#12878, #12905)
  • Esc must be pressed a second time to stop a running turn (#12859)
  • Extension surfaces support style fields, editable drafts, map-versus-dashboard views and non-blocking actions (#12830, #12854, #12876, #12896)
  • Installed packages can be searched by capability, show their contribution types, copy their invocation, and the install preview shows the resolved commit (#12065, #12117, #12070, #12075, #12098)
  • You can view a sub-agent's skill declarations and search an MCP server's tool list (#12102, #12085)
  • WSL distributions are discovered and one of them is probed (#12595)
  • The phone page can be added to the home screen; paired phones survive a restart, with a switch in settings (#12591, #12440 by @keeyangyy)
  • Linux arm64 .deb and .rpm packages are published (#12914)
  • Local stdio MCP servers receive the working directory and the host's read-only state (#12967)
  • The tool catalogue refreshes automatically when an MCP server adds or removes tools at runtime and sends a notification (#12138 by @boscocp)
  • The host's check of changed turns can be turned off (#12860)
  • Right-to-left text blocks are displayed in their own direction (#12118 by @xxoingr)
  • The workbench browser-tab menu gains "Close browser tabs to the right", reachable by keyboard (#12557 by @SivanCola)

Changed

  • The Versions page keeps only the latest few earlier versions and folds the rest behind a button (#12982)
  • Session lists use single-line ellipsis everywhere, and bulk selection moved to the "This machine" more menu (#12935 by @SivanCola)
  • The desktop sidebar uses divider bars for its actions, and the brand mark is the official wordmark throughout (#12918, #12936)
  • Computer use now runs on the CUA Driver (#12937)
  • The "Execution" block in a conversation is a faint single line, and its header sticks to the top when expanded (#12954, #12944)
  • The session title in the title bar sits next to the project name, and the branch button's hover shape matches its neighbours (#12955, #12971)
  • The model list keeps only the settings page's own scrollbar, and the display-alias input follows the theme (#12979)
  • Installed skills are grouped by source (#12329 by @KHG420)

Fixed

  • Folded user messages no longer vanish entirely when compaction cannot get a summary (#12963)
  • The context-usage figure now uses the same number the compaction trigger uses, instead of reading lower than the provider reports (#12991, #12988)
  • On Windows, clicking a task-completed notification brings the window to the front, and the click path is logged (#12907, #12956)
  • Closing a browser tab no longer reads content the kernel never sends (#12886)
  • A question you have started answering no longer times out and is skipped (#12884 by @par73e)
  • A turn in progress is kept when the runtime is rebuilt or a turn-end event is lost (#11735 by @jf200s)
  • Saving settings now reports success, not applied, and failure separately, and a failure can be retried (#12350 by @KHG420)
  • The undo entry is restored from the backend, and an expired-undo state is kept (#11589 by @Guan0923)
  • Unsaved file drafts in the workbench are kept (#12898 by @XTLine)
  • Context usage refreshes immediately after switching model settings (#12871 by @xlyy1)
  • Cost is shown in the wallet's currency, and the folder picker can list hidden folders (#12874)
  • On Windows, when the Git Bash probe hangs it is retried once, and the fallback shell path is shown correctly (#12887)
  • When only /skill-name is sent, history records exactly what you typed (#12890)
  • Duplicate MCP tool names are refused, and the original identifier stays the same on every path (#12889, #12893, #12892)
  • Sub-agent status frames are no longer lost when the client falls behind (#12888)
  • File preview checks that the target may be accessed, and passes through the PreToolUse hook first (#12115 by @Guan0923)
  • A failed memory save is rolled back, and a migrated memory repairs its index when changed again (#12938, #12939, #12940)

Full Changelog: https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/compare/studio-v2.38.0...studio-v2.39.0

macOS 已用 Developer ID 签名并公证，打开时不需要清除隔离属性。Windows 的窗口、内核与安装器都经 Certum 代码签名证书做了 Authenticode 签名。Linux Debian 系从 .deb 安装，Fedora、RHEL、openSUSE 等从 .rpm 安装（sudo dnf install ./ReasonixStudio-linux-amd64.rpm）。x86_64 用文件名带 amd64 的包，aarch64（统信 UOS、麒麟等）用带 arm64 的包。

三个平台都能自更新：Linux 的 .deb 走自己的包（.rpm 的自更新尚未提供，请手动下载新包），Windows 运行下一个安装器，macOS 替换自身 bundle。

macOS builds are signed with a Developer ID and notarized, so no quarantine attribute needs clearing. On Windows the window, kernel and installer are Authenticode-signed with a Certum code-signing certificate. On Linux, install the .deb on Debian-based systems and the .rpm on Fedora, RHEL, openSUSE and similar (sudo dnf install ./ReasonixStudio-linux-amd64.rpm). Use the amd64 file on x86_64 and the arm64 file on aarch64.

All three platforms self-update: the Linux .deb through its own package (the .rpm has no self-update yet, so download the new package by hand), Windows by running the next installer, and macOS by replacing its own bundle.

deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness ↗ 1 release · 1 prerelease
dsh-v0.2.1-alpha.3v0.2.1-alpha.3 Prerelease2026-10-14T13:32:15Z

中文 | English

<h3 id="cn-v0.2.1-alpha.3-community">新增功能</h3>

  • Web 版支持在本机 HTTP 访问时登录 DeepSeek 账号。 @lsdsjy
  • 支持识别已发送消息中的密钥和令牌，并默认脱敏按需查看。 @ZiyaZhang
  • 极简模式支持读取图片和自动压缩长对话，Web 与桌面端可通过 /compact 指令手动压缩上下文。 @LogosVeritas

问题修复

  • 修复切换会话、收起工作过程或发送后续消息时，对话区域出现大块空白、滚动跳动的问题。 @imccyu
  • 修复重启应用后部分会话无法继续，以及切换到 DeepSeek 后因历史消息兼容问题导致请求失败的情况。 @imccyu, @yudshj
  • 修复插件更新或重新安装后仍运行旧代码的问题。启用热重载时，可替换的插件会直接生效；无法热重载的情况会提示重启。 @turtle1999
  • 修复 macOS 添加工作区时目录选择器无法粘贴目录路径，以及取消选择仍添加工作区的问题。 @turtle2099
  • 修复原预设被删除或调整后，恢复空白会话、切换工作区可能失败的问题。 @LegGasai
  • 修复部分较旧 Git 版本下无法创建工作树的问题。 @turtle2099
  • 修复远程 Web 在网络拥塞时被误判为断线，以及刷新后语言、主题等偏好丢失的问题。 @turtle2099, @oraluben
  • 应用意外退出后，未完成的任务会明确显示“任务已停止”，避免被误认为正常完成。 @turtle2099
  • 凭据文件中出现 DSH 不使用的额外字段时，不再阻止应用启动。 @turtle2099
  • 修复卸载会话存储插件时，最后的会话记录可能丢失的问题。 @turtle1999

体验优化

  • 改善会话列表加载和 @ 会话搜索体验 @imccyu
  • 子智能体可通过配置选用独立 Agent Preset 预设，统一设置角色和工具； @grllll, @Dudu-0223
  • 优化侧栏展开规则，让运行中和等待回应的会话更容易找到；会话菜单支持复制会话 ID。 @turtle1999, @imccyu
  • DeepSeek 账号的模型列表随账号可用模型更新，并隐藏当前客户端版本不支持的模型。 @LegGasai
  • 模型设置更清楚地提示凭据来源和检测状态，支持按需显示输入的 API Key。 @Magolor
  • Office 文档任务按需加载编辑、渲染和导出指引，减少简单内容修改中不必要的检查与预览。 @yudshj
  • 改进子智能体的委派与等待指引：达到委派层数上限时提前提示，等待子任务期间不再消耗新的目标执行轮次。 @ZiyaZhang, @turtle1999
  • Web 终端更可靠地处理连续输入、粘贴和尺寸调整，并保留进程退出前的最后输出。 @LegGasai
  • 桌面端可用 Cmd/Ctrl + 加号或减号统一调整正文、代码和终端字号。 @koalazf99
  • 优化会话和工作区的悬停操作，统一卡片与菜单样式，并改善页头排列及插件页、自动化任务页的窄窗口布局。 @Yifffan, @Kingwl
  • PTC Mode 的 Node 执行环境新增可选的 in-memory 模式，多次运行代码可复用变量、函数和已导入模块。 @CreatixChu

其他变更

  • API Key 读取优先级变更：Web端/桌面端优先采用已保存的凭据；headless 默认改用环境变量和 .env，不读取已保存的密钥。 @Magolor
  • 更新第三方模型支持；Azure 提供方名称统一为 azure，使用旧名称 azure-openai-responses 的提供方配置需迁移。 @LegGasai
  • Python SDK 的 Office 引擎和完整创作环境改为按需下载。 @yudshj
  • 使用 --from-default-profile 重建已有自定义配置时，会替换该配置及其插件和设置。 @turtle2099
  • TypeScript 和 Python SDK 支持 resume 功能，恢复已保存的会话，继续使用原有会话 ID 和工作目录。 @koalazf99

<h3 id="en-v0.2.1-alpha.3-community">New Features</h3>

  • The Web app supports DeepSeek account sign-in when accessed over local HTTP. by @lsdsjy
  • Detect keys and tokens in sent messages and mask them by default, with an option to reveal them. by @ZiyaZhang
  • Minimal mode can read images and automatically compact long conversations. Web and Desktop users can run the /compact command to compact context manually. by @LogosVeritas

Bug Fixes

  • Fix large blank areas and scroll jumps when switching sessions, collapsing work details, or sending follow-up messages. by @imccyu
  • Fix failures when continuing some sessions after an application restart, and history compatibility errors when switching to DeepSeek. by @imccyu, @yudshj
  • Fix stale plugin code after updates or reinstallation. With hot reload enabled, replaceable plugins take effect immediately; changes that cannot be hot-reloaded request a restart. by @turtle1999
  • Fix pasting directory paths into the directory picker when adding a workspace on macOS, and prevent cancelled selections from adding a workspace. by @turtle2099
  • Fix failures when restoring a blank session or switching workspaces after its original preset was removed or changed. by @LegGasai
  • Fix worktree creation failures with some older Git versions. by @turtle2099
  • Fix false disconnects during network congestion and lost language, theme, and other preferences after refreshing a remote Web client. by @turtle2099, @oraluben
  • Tasks interrupted by an application exit now show “Task stopped” instead of appearing to have completed normally. by @turtle2099
  • Extra fields that DSH does not use in the credentials file no longer prevent the application from starting. by @turtle2099
  • Fix a case where final session records could be lost while unloading the session storage plugin. by @turtle1999

Improvements

  • Improve session list loading and @ session search. by @imccyu
  • Configure spawned subagents with independent Agent Presets to manage their roles and tools together. by @grllll, @Dudu-0223
  • Improve sidebar expansion so running sessions and sessions awaiting a response are easier to find. Session menus support copying the session ID. by @turtle1999, @imccyu
  • Keep the DeepSeek account model list up to date with the models available to the account, while hiding models unsupported by the current client version. by @LegGasai
  • Model settings show clearer credential sources and detection status, and let users reveal an entered API key. by @Magolor
  • Load Office editing, rendering, and export guidance as needed, reducing unnecessary checks and previews for simple content edits. by @yudshj
  • Improve subagent delegation and waiting guidance: announce delegation depth limits in advance and wait for running subtasks before starting another goal round. by @ZiyaZhang, @turtle1999
  • Handle continuous input, pastes, and resizing more reliably in the Web terminal, and preserve the final output when a process exits. by @LegGasai
  • Use Cmd/Ctrl + plus or minus on Desktop to adjust text, code, and terminal font sizes together. by @koalazf99
  • Refine session and workspace hover interactions, unify card and menu styling, and improve header ordering and narrow-window layouts on plugin and automation pages. by @Yifffan, @Kingwl
  • The Node runtime for PTC Mode adds an optional in-memory mode that reuses variables, functions, and imported modules across code executions. by @CreatixChu

Chores

  • Change API key precedence: Web and Desktop prioritize saved credentials. Headless uses environment variables and .env files by default, without reading saved keys. by @Magolor
  • Update third-party model support and rename the Azure provider to azure. Provider configurations using azure-openai-responses must be migrated. by @LegGasai
  • The Python SDK’s Office engine and full authoring environment are now downloaded on demand. by @yudshj
  • Using --from-default-profile to recreate an existing custom profile replaces that profile, including its plugins and settings. by @turtle2099
  • The TypeScript and Python SDKs support session resume, restoring saved sessions with their original session IDs and working directories. by @koalazf99

Full Changelog: dsh-v0.2.1-alpha.2...dsh-v0.2.1-alpha.3

cua-driver-rs-v0.35.4cua-driver-rs: v0.35.4 Prerelease2026-10-14T12:32:14Z

Cua Driver 0.35.4

Summary

This release includes 16 fixes.

Fixes

  • print the named command's help for <command> --help. (#5083) Thanks @RitikaxG, @injaneity; reported by @coygeek.
  • wake Chromium AT-SPI content before the first application tree traversal. (#5074)
  • refuse background Cmd+V when the browser focus is its omnibox. (#5124)
  • keep list_windows valid when macOS reports non-finite window bounds. (#5125) Thanks @injaneity, @kayzenline.
  • background browser clicks reach pointer-driven rows. (#5110)
  • share one approved Chrome connection across sessions. (#5115)
  • keep the Chromium screen rebase off a top-level frame that matches its window-manager frame. (#4967) Thanks @CMGS.
  • stop opening a console window for the embedded daemon on Windows. (#5136) Thanks @SunflowersLwtech.
  • keep background popups and context menus open on macOS. (#5129)
  • fail type_text when a character cannot be typed. (#5139) Thanks @CMGS.
  • preserve macOS middle-click window routing. (#5137) Thanks @Iflaqbhat.
  • exit 64 for an unknown permissions subcommand. (#5148) Thanks @UlviShukurzade.
  • preserve recording start timestamp in session.json. (#5145) Thanks @Iflaqbhat.
  • isolate Computer History under CUA_DRIVER_RS_HOME. (#5165) Thanks @AyinLee987.
  • give each character of a text its own spare keycode. (#5158) Thanks @CMGS.
  • write action.json timestamp as ISO-8601 as documented. (#5144) Thanks @methanoya.

Contributors

Thanks to @AyinLee987, @CMGS, @coygeek, @Iflaqbhat, @kayzenline, @methanoya, @RitikaxG, @SunflowersLwtech, @UlviShukurzade for contributing to this release.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

GitHub's label is used only to keep this monorepo's repository-wide “Latest” pointer from switching between independently released products. A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

1.0.0 (2026-10-13)

⚠ BREAKING CHANGES

  • cli: rtk proxy <cmd> is now rtk bypass <cmd> (the old name stays as an alias), --ultra-compact is removed, rtk gain excludes oversized outputs from its headline rate, and the default binary's licence changes (an Apache-2.0 edition, rtk-oss, ships alongside). See BREAKING_CHANGES.md.

Features

  • cli: pipelines in the CLI, hook gate, rtk llm-proxy (5d8102a)
  • engine: pipeline engine, plugin and text crates (62e9047)
  • hooks: agent plugins honour pipeline block and route (380a804)
  • proxy: rtk-proxy daemon, pipelines at the request layer (8420c11)

Bug Fixes

  • hooks: pipeline hook rules act on Claude Code only; the shared hook core is develop's again (2b96061)
  • pipeline: route before an edit is refused; the hook load rejects tasks named after real commands; a blank message keeps a reason (45d6ba7)
  • release: one release and one v-tag for every crate (e61317a)

v0.97.0 Stable2026-10-14T13:14:55Z

Highlights

  • Use custom HTTP JSON search providers with configurable authentication, result mapping, and fallback engines
  • Rust and TypeScript SDK clients can invoke native runtime commands and transfer sessions without turning commands into agent prompts

Improvements

  • Dismiss side panels directly from the TUI, and keep routine system notices on a compact single line
  • Default opt-out usage telemetry can now include the active Anthropic or OpenAI OAuth account email when the matching server rollout is available, with a conditional one-time TUI notice, 90-day retention, and collection controls in /telemetry

Fixes

  • Azure agent turns use the Responses API with correct route selection, explicit reasoning settings, and more reliable tool-call and retry handling
  • Anthropic model catalog requests include the required authentication headers
  • Browser handoff failures preserve reconnect timeout diagnostics
  • Empty math no longer triggers LaTeX rendering, and repeated fallback failures avoid duplicate logging

Full changelog: https://github.com/1jehuang/jcode/compare/v0.96.0...v0.97.0

Release source recovery

Built from existing tag v0.97.0 with reviewed windows-listener repair from workflow commit f8c8e6c5db60aea1c40c317175c56759bb437bef. The tag and version are unchanged. This is patched tag source, not the exact tagged tree. Release-only installer tooling is pinned to the same workflow commit. See release-provenance.json for source hashes and build run.

v0.96.0 Stable2026-10-14T11:11:19Z

Highlights

  • Connect to remote MCP servers over HTTP or SSE, including OAuth sign-in and command-line management
  • Choose saved OAuth accounts per session from the model and login pickers, with email labels and cancelable account-switch countdowns
  • SDK clients can organize sessions into collections and attach hidden reminders to individual turns

Improvements

  • Swarm messages use compact, expandable one-row previews, while the activity dock shows session icons and each worker's current todo step
  • Pinned todos span the full terminal width
  • The /reset usage limits claude command supports resetting Claude session limits
  • Prompts and pokes appear in every window attached to a session
  • New-model usage costs receive estimates before published pricing becomes available

Fixes

  • The first pinned todo update no longer hides the original session prompt, and hidden todo updates preserve transcript cache boundaries
  • OpenAI native web citations render source links reliably without losing links already shown
  • Provider failover honors account pools and model preferences, rotates rejected OAuth accounts, and stops retrying permanently rejected sessions
  • Stale Anthropic usage is marked unavailable instead of showing misleading limits
  • Browser control identifies the correct browser bridge and explains silent Chrome extension connections
  • Remote MCP handshakes are bounded so unresponsive servers do not hold up startup indefinitely
  • Copy-selection Enter and Y shortcuts work in client mode, and Ctrl+L keeps the fresh view when new messages arrive
  • Escaped short prose lists no longer render as math, and system-notice emoji no longer leave ghost terminal cells
  • SDK-launched sessions no longer flash console windows inside Windows GUI applications

Full changelog: https://github.com/1jehuang/jcode/compare/v0.95.0...v0.96.0

Release source recovery

This is patched tag source, not the exact tagged tree. Release-only installer tooling is pinned to the same workflow commit. See release-provenance.json for source hashes and build run.

rea-agents-6.5.0rea-agents: v6.5.0 Stable2026-10-14T12:32:45Z

6.5.0 (2026-10-13)

Provider prerequisites and recorded verification scope are listed in the testing guide.

Highlights

  • Analyze selected JavaScript files and recover cross-bundle relationships without expanding the selected source scope.
  • Read compact summaries and page retained Evidence across binary, managed, Android, Dart, WASM and network workflows.
  • Inspect SQLite snapshots, packet and signal recordings, WebAssembly, Rust dependency metadata and Electron fuses offline.
  • Keep completed Evidence and partial observations available when analysis or cleanup fails.

Changes

  • JavaScript source selection: analyze exact application paths without expanding imports into the selected scope. Unselected files retain identity and explicit unknown coverage; summary delivery remains separate from source selection. (#1944, issue morluto/rea#1848). Thanks @kaoru0822-kitauji, @N0zoM1z0.

  • Code-split recovery: recover related bundles from multiple selected inputs, retaining per-file provenance and downstream cross-chunk imports. Recorded CLI/MCP coverage is Linux x64 with Wakaru 1.14.0 and source-owned esbuild/webpack fixtures; other hosts, releases and arbitrary application equivalence remain unverified. (#1686, issue morluto/rea#1605). Thanks @JingHao-Leon, @pionxzh.

  • Package imports: resolve exact package import aliases using the selected loader and package ancestry, rather than guessing sibling files. (#1862, issue morluto/rea#1838). Thanks @AWUQAQ, @mvanhorn.

  • Retained analysis views: opt into compact inventory/WASM, managed metadata, Android manifest, Dart AOT, Mach-O and EVM summaries, then page retained Evidence without rerunning analysis. Summary delivery preserves the complete recorded result and its original coverage. (#2122, #2193, #2413, #2438, #2455, #2634, #2308, 9a18251). Thanks @kaoru0822-kitauji, @N0zoM1z0.

  • Managed assemblies: Select declaring types when inspecting members, decode inspected ldstr literals with independent coverage, and page retained member summaries. Call and field targets expose target_declaring_type and field_declaring_type, including supported generic method targets; unresolved metadata remains unknown. (#1706, #2125, issue morluto/rea#2121, #2162, issue morluto/rea#2158, #3061, issue morluto/rea#3010, #2163, issue morluto/rea#2160). Thanks @kaoru0822-kitauji, @N0zoM1z0, @Nelglor, @skoodster.

  • Packet captures: inspect saved packets with bounded TShark output, compact capture summaries and retained pages; discover installed protocol/field definitions. Recorded real-engine coverage uses Linux and Wireshark 4.6.6; native Windows/macOS and Bun behavior remain unverified. (#2318, #2340, #2496). Thanks @3QL, @N0zoM1z0, @Olorinm.

  • Recorded signals: decode existing recordings and discover installed decoders/options through caller-supplied sigrok-cli 0.7.2. This is Linux-only and requires util-linux prlimit; it acquires no hardware samples. (#2339, #2487). Thanks @N0zoM1z0, @Olorinm.

  • Web captures: derive an inferred method/origin/path-template API surface from retained HAR data including observed query-parameter names while retaining unknowns; query values, headers and bodies remain excluded. (#1891, #2982, issue morluto/rea#2913). Thanks @mvanhorn, @wunianze666-netizen, @zhangguiping-xydt.

  • QUIC captures: inspect retained aioquic 1.5.0 JSON qlog 0.3 objects and page traces/streams/observations. Other qlog layouts remain unsupported; overlap and loss declarations do not prove retransmission or network loss. (#2277). Thanks @LuisTaffelli, @N0zoM1z0.

  • Fiddler captures: inspect native SAZ archives with retained HTTP provenance and bounded decoding. Recorded producer coverage is the pinned public encoding corpus; current Fiddler Everywhere variants and Bun-native execution remain unverified. (#2409). Thanks @N0zoM1z0, @zahhak.

  • SQLite snapshots: inspect an explicitly selected offline database with table/column selection and bounded Evidence. This operation requires Node 24.x >=24.15 or 26+ with DatabaseSync.limits; supported Node 22 and earlier 24 runtimes cannot use it. A live database/WAL copy does not establish an atomic snapshot. (#2271). Thanks @N0zoM1z0, @Y0UW1SH.

  • WASM artifacts: Inspect local modules with bring-your-own WABT 1.0.42 without executing them. Compare imports/exports from two complete core-module inspection Evidence records without requiring an active WABT installation; unsupported signatures and duplicate identities remain unknown, and comparisons do not establish runtime compatibility. Recorded real-tool inspection coverage is macOS arm64. (#1533, issue morluto/rea#884, #2464, issue morluto/rea#2356). Thanks @matbrgz, @N0zoM1z0, @thangldw.

  • Go binaries: inspect embedded build metadata, including file-backed unmapped Mach-O debug segments. (#1363, 7fb097d). Thanks @Y0UW1SH.

  • Flutter APKs: identify snapshot/engine builds and inspect AOT structure without an external engine. Recorded real APK coverage uses the documented Flutter Gallery fixture; macOS/Windows and complete pool semantics remain unverified, and pool coverage stays partial. (#1582). Thanks @3QL.

  • HarmonyOS packages: inventory HAP/HSP/App Pack structures without executing code. The recorded real workflow covers one pinned HAP through repository-built CLI/MCP; installed-package and real HSP/App Pack coverage remain unverified. (#1447). Thanks @BaaRai.

  • Ghidra raw inputs and references: compatible Linux/macOS Ghidra profiles accept caller-selected raw architecture/entry information and expose typed incoming references at exact addresses. Windows Ghidra P0 admits the raw NMOS 6502 profile described below; other raw targets and the incoming-reference facet remain outside Windows P0. (#1868, issue morluto/rea#1836, #1715, issue morluto/rea#1245). Thanks @bugstan, @casesolved-co-uk, @mvanhorn, @N0zoM1z0.

  • Ghidra MIPS: Add bounded MIPS32r2/o32 relocatable objects, soft-float integer-register storage, admitted MIPS16 shared ELF, and a narrowly admitted recordless legacy MIPS32 executable profile. The latter pins an endian-matched 32-bit language and records inferred o32 as an analysis assumption. Admission follows each documented ABI/flag/relocation boundary. (#1949, issue morluto/rea#1937, #1950, issue morluto/rea#1938, #1991, issue morluto/rea#1851, #1898, issue morluto/rea#1620). Thanks @AurelioB, @BaaRai, @bqw371, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @N0zoM1z0.

  • Ghidra RISC-V: admit little-endian RV32/RV64 ET_EXEC ELF declaring the supported double-float ABI. Relocatable/shared, big-endian and other ABI profiles remain refused; see the profile and recorded lane. (#2151). Thanks @NIU-123370.

  • PSP static ELF profile: the Ghidra profile is Linux x64, bounded little-endian ELF32 ET_EXEC with the documented PSP/ABI declarations and a compatible caller-installed Allegrex extension. PRX, shared/relocatable images, PBP/ISO/CSO and runtime emulation are outside scope. Historical extension experiments are separate from public-provider verification; real public Ghidra PSP coverage remains unverified. (#1599). Thanks @jaelliot.

  • Ghidra analysis settings: apply validated analyzer overrides before analysis while retaining the selected analysis profile. (#1913). Thanks @akushonkamen, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @N0zoM1z0.

  • Attached native IDA MCP: add an optional native bridge alongside existing legacy/headless profiles. Recorded native runs use Linux IDA Free 9.4 with a navigation correction and Windows IDA Pro 9.3 with a loopback-listener change; stock native switching, native macOS and Windows IDA Free remain unverified. Attached databases remain read-only and are never saved or closed. (#1544). Thanks @imissapixel, @N0zoM1z0.

  • Frida instrumentation: add explicit local/remote target selection, caller-supplied scripts and retained messages, with script-read/input diagnostics and bounded message eviction. Recorded real coverage is the Linux local Node marker workflow; macOS, Windows and remote devices/servers remain unverified. Instrumentation executes foreign code and is destructive, not read-only. (#1359, #2211, issue morluto/rea#2209, #2217, issue morluto/rea#2213, #2628). Thanks @dandgabr, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @N0zoM1z0.

  • Client setup: register Qoder, Trae, ZCode, DeepSeek Harness, Kimi Code, Factory Droid and WorkBuddy through the existing scoped setup flow. DeepSeek is included from 6.5.0; the historical npm 6.4 check did not cover it. Live Trae and DeepSeek API integration remain unverified. (#1564, #1572, #1573, #1929, issue morluto/rea#1839, #2744, #2309, #3041, issue morluto/rea#3040). Thanks @0xRafie, @BaaRai, @cainiao1992, @IvenKooLab, @whoishaha, @ysntony.

  • Website: Add maintained language editions, persistent/system-aware themes, keyboard styling and nested Evidence indentation, plus the reverse-engineering models essay. A simpler home page and beginner journey connect platform prerequisites, client/provider setup and a supplied first investigation. (#1779, issue morluto/rea#1022, #1699, #1714, #1894, #2250, #2432, #2732, #3274). Thanks @CassieFox23, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @N0zoM1z0.

  • Rust dependency metadata: Inspect embedded cargo-auditable package/dependency records in ELF, PE and thin Mach-O without executing the file. Missing metadata and inferred compiler hashes retain explicit limitations; universal Mach-O requires a selected thin slice. (#2456). Thanks @mvanhorn.

  • Electron fuses: Read packaged fuse bytes and per-slice runtime implications without launching or modifying the app. Missing, truncated and unknown wires remain explicit; fuse observations do not prove other Inspector-enablement paths. (#2399). Thanks @kaoru0822-kitauji, @mvanhorn, @N0zoM1z0.

  • Ghidra PowerPC: Admit bounded ELF32 big-endian ET_EXEC PowerPC with an explicit language profile on supported Linux/macOS hosts. PPC64, DOL and other variants remain outside this lane; see the profile and verification prerequisites. (#1747). Thanks @arielfikru.

  • Reference-source summaries: Use CLI import-reference-source --detail summary for compact manifest, count, state and path-outline output. The complete graph remains available and is still the default; hidden outline entries retain separate coverage counts. (#2768, issue morluto/rea#2442). Thanks @matbrgz, @To3akaRin.

  • Recorded AArch64 crashes: Decode 34 ABI register values per thread from supported Linux ELF64 little-endian core notes using the documented caller-provided Python dependencies. These are recorded facts, not proof of current process or library identity. (#2505, issue morluto/rea#2353). Thanks @matbrgz, @NIU-123370.

  • Ghidra byte search: Search initialized analyzed memory using wildcards, bit masks, address bounds and paged results. Unloaded, overlay and uninitialized blocks retain skipped coverage; this is not a raw-file search. (#2341). Thanks @mvanhorn.

  • Raw NMOS 6502 images: Analyze explicitly selected headerless images with Ghidra, including Windows P0, using a caller-selected entry and source mapping within the 16-bit address space. Cartridge parsing, reset-vector inference and 65C02/65816 are outside this profile. (#2079). Thanks @Zhong-fan.

  • Swift String observations: Ghidra function dossiers derive supported AArch64 Swift String literals from constant-register pairs at recognized use sites, retaining instruction/register provenance and explicit omissions. This is a bounded derivation, not a complete string inventory. (#2049, issue morluto/rea#2030). Thanks @thefolahan.

  • Headless IDA procedure lookup: Resolve addresses to canonical containing-procedure entries and identify the session’s admitted program document in the modern headless profile. Incomplete surveys remain errors; attached profiles do not advertise this containment capability. (#2374, issue morluto/rea#2335). Thanks @nikolay-dementiev.

  • Passive browser attachment: Six existing passive browser tools accept an explicit-port loopback browser WebSocket endpoint when HTTP target discovery is unavailable. Existing consent and target/origin checks remain required; this does not launch a browser or enable runtime instrumentation. (#2346). Thanks @all-black-493.

  • Android DEX references: Retain exact method descriptors and individual direct-invoke observations with source method, DEX entry, opcode and code-unit offset. These are static references; unavailable instruction facts remain separate from method-level usage totals. (#2439). Thanks @LcokHcak.

  • Offline EVM metadata: Retain bounded, lossless facts from plausible terminal Solidity CBOR root-map candidates. Candidates do not prove Solidity origin or source/compiler identity, and linked metadata is not fetched. (#2790). Thanks @matbrgz.

Fixes

  • JavaScript loader and export identity: Respect import/require conditions and unavailable package metadata, keep export links separate from writes, and resolve default identifier exports to the value captured at the export statement. (7e5cffa, 322506d, 81f7638, dae1e55). Thanks @paoValle, @VarunGore36, @xu91102.

  • JavaScript traces: Retain nested bridge calls and surviving early-return predicates. Honor node-ID match options, match route seeds against source values, and keep ambiguous runtime candidates unknown. (0351328, 5031099, 44c9d29, 5e79d46). Thanks @jpdefo.

  • Browser comparison coverage: Compare only known redirect response fields and retain network-selection recovery guidance alongside independent event changes. Missing response facts do not prove equality or a change. (2b60e09).

  • Evidence comparison and ledger input: Report display-only bundle differences as unchanged while retaining digests, and classify noncanonical bundle records as invalid caller input. (030c477, 3a34ad3).

  • Capture and admission cleanup: Keep deleted-file observations and original bytes when a parent becomes a file, preserve unrelated I/O failures during cancellation, and require explicit pre-launch proof before releasing an unknown LLDB target owner. LLDB failure controls used simulated boundaries. (e67c364, 2daeff4, 45b6a7f). Thanks @SII-YDD, @xu91102.

  • Client configuration: Validate MCP output-schema profiles before setup writes, ignore untrusted Gemini workspace settings before parsing them, and identify installed skills independently of marker-only client roots. (0930e94, aade9bb, ba33c70). Thanks @CmsChase, @paoValle, @sx4im.

  • Host tool discovery: Share macOS Hopper launcher discovery across setup and runtime, preserve explicit paths, and report validated ADB and Apktool availability. (7582f0d, e5deb83). Thanks @Chessing234, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @viniciusdsandrade.

  • SAZ encoding validation: Reject invalid XML bytes, unsupported encodings and declarations that contradict the archive bytes, including records outside the selected request. (1250aae).

  • Managed member matching: Keep malformed TypeDef list ownership unknown, distinguish constants during structural method matching, and accept legacy small exception-section sizes. (3f6b575, ee5cfbe).

  • Browser observations: preserve effective source-map URLs, empty query/fragment identity, response-body provenance, service-worker policies and explicit uncertainty for malformed events. Bound discovery lifetimes and retain cleanup ownership. Interrupt/retry/failure cases validated with controlled CDP/session fixtures do not establish those paths in real Chromium. (#1742, issue morluto/rea#1729, #1829, #2074, #2099, #2239, issue morluto/rea#2169, #2477, 486b567, a82e37c, a8d4984, 966f7cc, 221c856, 951b095, f09bb8a, 6bd2b28). Thanks @1052326311, @chAng-L19, @chqcjq, @coygeek, @evenafterallisaid, @fisforfaheem, @itsF4LCON, @N0zoM1z0, @shayanxcyber.

  • Managed metadata/CIL: preserve ExportedType and pointer-table ownership, generic/array/function-pointer/modifier signatures, ReadyToRun host identity, metadata ExtraData and signed zero. Charge retained facts and decoded instruction counts instead of broad decoding bounds; malformed layouts remain caller input errors. (#1743, issue morluto/rea#1736, #1768, #1822, issue morluto/rea#1801, #1828, issue morluto/rea#1812, #1835, issue morluto/rea#1832, #1886, #1893, #1897, issue morluto/rea#1878, #1943, issue morluto/rea#1940, #1985, #2194, issue morluto/rea#2191, #2437, 52e76c5). Thanks @ac1982, @chqcjq, @fisHarly0, @flitzrrr, @JasonBuildAI, @JayOfTheKeyboard, @jhuang-tw, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @N0zoM1z0, @Nelglor.

  • Hopper failure ownership: preserve startup/process outcomes, retain cleanup leases across one-shot failures, wait for late bridges, and stop owned Linux demo children when their REA owner exits. Denied/crashed/late-bridge/queued-request failure paths use simulated process or bridge fixtures; they are not fresh installed-Hopper verification. (#1763, issue morluto/rea#1751, #1817, #1866, #1927, #2474, #2499, issue morluto/rea#2459). Thanks @coygeek, @Joshwong1908, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @N0zoM1z0, @rudycelekli.

  • IDA session boundaries: retain non-x86 call coverage and unresolved-procedure uncertainty, and bound private universal-Mach-O inputs to the exact selected slice. Transient headless busy recovery was exercised on macOS arm64 IDA Pro 9.1; Linux headless, attached GUI navigation and worker-reaped cleanup paths retain their documented verification gaps. (#1764, issue morluto/rea#1712, #1778, issue morluto/rea#1766, #1785, issue morluto/rea#1769, #1906, issue morluto/rea#1794, #2201, issue morluto/rea#2139, #2291, issue morluto/rea#2285, #2324, 12fa8fd). Thanks @akushonkamen, @coygeek, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @N0zoM1z0, @nikolay-dementiev, @zecaro.

  • Input and capability diagnostics: identify invalid paths, format refusals, missing host tools and non-retryable errors without losing caller selections or retryable capability facts. Foreign execution/device mutation annotations remain destructive, analysis-target text is untrusted, and output ordering follows code points rather than host locale. (#1770, #1867, #1869, issue morluto/rea#1855, #1872, #1879, issue morluto/rea#1853, #1899, #1902, #1904, #1908, #1925, #1987, issue morluto/rea#1979, #2004, issue morluto/rea#2002, #2026, #2091, #2133, issue morluto/rea#2129, #2145, issue morluto/rea#2143, #2472, #2478, issue morluto/rea#2454, #2494, #2507, #2509, #2708, 3d83135, c9fed50, 8b18171, 7a2cd1e). Thanks @astralyx0129, @CassieFox23, @DeryFerd, @dollarsmoney, @fisforfaheem, @flitzrrr, @Ha1baraA11, @jimpablo, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @larinyo, @N0zoM1z0, @rudycelekli, @SII-YDD, @viniciusdsandrade, @XiaoBinGan.

  • Android analysis: retain decoded values, duplicate-class inventories and executable static initializers, reject malformed JADX UTF-8, and keep inline search provenance. Apktool accepts --no-strings and explains host requirements. Windows batch-launcher selection also resolves PATH/PATHEXT before choosing its launch mode. Recorded Apktool coverage is Linux/OpenJDK, and changed JADX/batch failure paths do not establish native Windows behavior. (#1777, issue morluto/rea#1774, #1876, issue morluto/rea#1846, #1890, issue morluto/rea#1850, #1946, issue morluto/rea#1945, #2707, issue morluto/rea#2672, 0677468, fe8d0bd, #2802). Thanks @akushonkamen, @coygeek, @HeadmasterEggy, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @N0zoM1z0, @psipher.

  • ADB acquisition: preserve device paths and directory identity, prevent host destination collisions/overwrites, count UTF-8 logcat bytes and restricted permissions, and evaluate the complete Activity Manager start report. Launch attempts, status, activity and raw failure output remain observations; real-device timeout/mixed-report and Android 8 directory-read paths remain unverified beyond the documented fixtures. (#1962, #2212, issue morluto/rea#2175, #2221, issue morluto/rea#2220, #2359, c42232f, 55b6148, 3bdc873, c89972c, e1cd292). Thanks @coygeek, @dakjdakd, @DeryFerd, @huiq777, @sx4im.

  • WASM names and diagnostics: preserve debug/custom-section names including NUL and leading BOM, accept unpadded WABT section rows, and avoid irrelevant tool-remediation advice on unrelated runtime-root failures. (#1963, issue morluto/rea#1956, #1967, #1981, #2084, #2102). Thanks @1052326311, @bugstan, @N0zoM1z0, @sathariels, @STiFLeR7, @viniciusdsandrade.

  • Source-map and path handling: retain source-map entries when embedded-content counts differ or an optional sourceMappingURL is used, and keep file: URLs out of operator-relative path resolution. (#2873, #2886, #2882, issue morluto/rea#2821, issue morluto/rea#2883, issue morluto/rea#2577). Thanks @JFSAS, @rileygramlich, @shoyann, @shubhamahuja9999, @coygeek.

  • Native metadata: accept declared facts in native dispatch inventories and share native verification input diagnostics. (#2906, #2901, issue morluto/rea#2564, issue morluto/rea#2621). Thanks @ShitK, @coygeek.

⚠ BREAKING CHANGES

  • Runtime attachment state: attached may be null in JavaScript, browser and Electron discovery/inspection results. Treat null as unknown; do not coerce it to false. (e55ef72).

  • Source maps: original-source and mapping rows preserve a null source name when unavailable. Handle null explicitly instead of expecting an invented unknown-source string; new index fields remain optional when reading older Evidence. (ee50ed1).

  • Streamed filtered JSON: --filter-output now emits JSON null instead of {} when the selected value is null; consumers must distinguish a present null from a missing selection. Use --full-output when an object envelope is required. (#2333). Thanks @Jackmeson1.

  • Call graphs: get_call_graph.normalized_result is now { graph, limitations }; read traversal-depth groups under graph and retain relationship-specific caveats. Contract/fixture validation does not establish new Ghidra or IDA engine coverage. (5dfb9d0).

  • MCP discovery: binary_session no longer repeats server_identity.catalog.tools. Use the connected server tool list, grouped tool_availability and provider capabilities; see MCP contracts. Origin: 6395e8d. (#1988). Thanks @kaoru0822-kitauji, @N0zoM1z0.

  • Provider admission: MIPS targets with unresolved GNU ABI attributes are refused. Supply supported ABI declarations. Saved Hopper databases on macOS are unavailable because the launcher cannot run the analysis bridge when opening a database; select an executable target instead. Database support requires a script-capable database launch route. (d4bd65c).

  • Segment extents: binary_overview segment length may be null when Hopper coordinates cannot establish a finite extent in one address space. Preserve unknown rather than converting it to zero or a saturated value. (36db19c).

  • Invocation identity: server.command_path may be null, and registration/setup statuses or doctor classifications may be unknown. Preserve these states and establish the actual invocation before treating a registration as aligned. (187f113). Thanks @hemv-857, @qorvr.

  • ADB installation: the optional replace input is unchanged. Results now require replace_requested to record the requested adb install -r mode; replaced is nullable because prior device package state was not observed. Update result readers to accept null instead of treating reinstall intent as proof of replacement. (864314a).

  • Managed/native verification: unresolved P/Invoke results may retain matched_native and candidate facts when module identity is unknown. Interpret status, basis and limitations; module-identity-unknown does not verify correspondence, and unresolved no longer implies empty observations. (3f6b575).

Full contribution record: 893 commits and 529 merged PRs.
