farion1231/cc-switch ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v4.0.12CC Switch v4.0.12 Stable2026-10-14T10:58:28Z

CC Switch v4.0.12

本版本让「应用」页可以完整管理编程工具：安装、升级、卸载，以及检测安装冲突。同一个工具装了多处时，现在可以直接选择卸载多余的那一处，冲突在 CC Switch 里就能解决；升级前会先检查 Node 版本，新版本要求更高的 Node 时不再显示注定失败的升级按钮；Grok Build 新版的安装方式也能卸载了。另外新增「本地」分组，可以直接添加在本机运行的 Ollama、LM Studio、llama.cpp、vLLM，并新增 Ollama Cloud、Groq、Hugging Face、Vercel AI Gateway 等 14 家云平台的预设。会话日志的后台扫描从每 60 秒一次改为每 10 分钟一次，平时更省资源；打开用量页时按所选间隔扫描，新用量反而显示得更快。界面新增土耳其语（社区翻译）；系统语言不在支持范围内时，首次启动改用英文。还修复了云同步恢复 Skills 失败一次后再也恢复不了、Claude Code 经路由连接 Responses 上游时回复结束后又报错等问题。4.0 完整的更新内容请见 v4.0 发布说明。

English → | 日本語版 →

新增

「应用」页：安装、升级、卸载、检测安装冲突都齐了。 v4.0.11 加上卸载之后，这一版补齐了剩下的环节。一个工具装了多处时，「诊断安装冲突」会列出每一处，并标出命令行实际使用的「默认」那处；现在卸载时可以选择其中任意一处，留下想用的那份，冲突直接在 CC Switch 里解决。升级前会先核对 Node 版本，不再给出装不上、或者装完跑不起来的升级。

卸载时可以选择卸载哪一处安装。 以前确认框只提供当前使用的那一处，而装了多处的用户往往想卸载的是另一处；有多处安装又认不出哪处是默认时，卸载会直接失败。现在确认框会列出检测到的每一处安装，带来源、路径、版本和「默认」标记：只有一处能卸载时自动选中，有两处以上时不预选，不能自动卸载的显示原因并禁用。执行前按你选的路径重新核对一遍，和预览不一致就不执行。

  • 支持 Grok Build 0.2.112 起的安装方式（~/.grok/bin/grok-<version>），以前会提示「无法自动卸载」。卸载会删除这套安装的全部程序文件，只删文件不删目录，config.toml 和会话保留。
  • 卸载官方脚本装的工具时，指向同一程序的启动链接（例如 ~/.local/bin/grok）会一起删除，不再留下失效的命令。
  • 已有官方安装、又在 ~/.grok/config.toml 里设置用 npm 更新时，npm 那一份会被 grok update 装回来，确认框会提示这一点。
  • Windows 上卸载 Hermes 时，确认框会提示正在运行的 Python 可能被锁定而留下文件；卸载命令成功但随后的状态检查失败时，改为提示「卸载命令已完成，请检查结果」，不再误报卸载失败。

升级前检查 Node 版本。 OpenClaw 2026.9.3 起要求 Node 24.16 以上，旧版 Node 上升级必然失败，「应用」页却一直显示升级按钮；没有自带检查的 npm 包更糟，npm 只警告一声就装上，装完运行不了。现在发现新版本时会先读取它要求的 Node 版本，和这处安装实际使用的 Node 比较。不满足时这一行写明需要先升级 Node，不显示升级按钮，「全部升级」也会跳过它；执行升级前还会再查一次。认不出的版本写法、查询失败都不会挡住升级；原生程序、Homebrew、Bun 安装和 WSL 里的安装不做这项检查。

「应用」页更整齐。 卸载从每行的红色按钮移进行尾的「…」菜单。表头和各行共用同一套列宽，版本列对齐了；读取版本、发现更新、检查更新期间，表头和各列不再左右跳动（#8293）。隐藏应用的说明改写得更明确：只是不显示，供应商、MCP 等配置都还在，它自己的配置文件也不会被改动。

本地推理服务：Ollama、LM Studio、llama.cpp、vLLM。 添加供应商时多了一个「本地」分组，放在「云服务商」之后，里面是连接本机推理服务的预设，默认地址就是各服务的默认端口（Ollama 11434、LM Studio 1234、llama.cpp 9931、vLLM 8000），llama.cpp 旧版、Homebrew 和 Docker 常用的 8080 端口作为备选地址。这些服务默认不校验 Key，预设里填的是各家文档用的占位值。使用前要先启动服务、下载模型，预设里的默认模型可以换成你本地已有的，也可以点「获取模型列表」从服务读取；上下文窗口以启动服务时设定的长度为准。

  • Claude Code、Codex 直接连本地服务的 Anthropic Messages 和 Responses 接口，Claude Desktop 经路由连接；OpenCode、OpenClaw、Hermes、Pi 按各自已有的内置供应商情况提供（应用自带了的就不重复添加）。
  • 发往本机地址（localhost、127.0.0.1 等）的请求不再走 CC Switch 设置的代理或系统代理。以前设置了代理时，路由转发、测速、获取模型这些请求会被送进代理，连不上本地服务。

预设： 新增 Ollama Cloud、Baseten、Meta Model API、DeepInfra、Vercel AI Gateway、Nebius Token Factory、Groq、SambaNova、DigitalOcean、Hugging Face、Microsoft Foundry、Fireworks AI、Mistral AI、Cloudflare AI Gateway；OpenCode 新增硅基流动国内站和国际站（#4500）；StreamLake 新增 Codex 预设，所有 StreamLake 预设改用公开的 KAT-Coder 接入点，不用再自己创建接入点 ID。

改进

会话日志扫描：后台更省资源，用量页更及时。 以前不管用不用得上，后台都每 60 秒把会话日志扫一遍，长时间开着 CC Switch 时，这部分读盘和 CPU 一直在消耗；而用量页的「自动刷新间隔」只是重新查一遍数据库，会话用量仍然要等这 60 秒，选 5 秒也没用（#8301）。现在这个设置改名为「会话日志扫描间隔」：打开用量页时立即扫描一次，窗口重新显示时再扫一次，之后按所选间隔（5、10、30、60 秒，默认 60 秒）扫描；后台扫描改为每 10 分钟一次，只作兜底，平时的后台消耗明显减少。「关闭」选项去掉了，关闭「自动扫描会话日志」时不显示这个设置。经路由的用量本来就是实时记录的，不受影响。

供应商搜索框不再遮挡或推开卡片。 搜索框以前浮在列表上方，会挡住第一张卡片的编辑和更多操作按钮（#8100）。现在它放在卡片上方那一行的最右端：Claude Code、Codex、Gemini CLI、Grok Build 放在模式那一行，其他应用放在「已添加」标题那一行。打开搜索时卡片既不被挡住，也不会被往下推。

界面语言。 新增土耳其语，由社区翻译，还没翻译的文字显示英文（#8208）。首次启动时，如果系统语言是界面不支持的语言（德语、法语、韩语等），界面和托盘改用英文，不再默认简体中文；已经选过的语言不受影响。

修复

  • 云同步恢复 Skills 失败一次之后，再也恢复不了（#8153）。Windows 上下载云同步时如果遇到文件被占用（os error 5），会留下 skills.bak，之后每次恢复都在处理它时失败。现在恢复时把云端的 Skills 完整复制到单独的目录再整体替换，不再复用 skills.bak；替换和回滚都失败时保留原来的完整副本，并在报错里给出它的位置。
  • Claude Code 经路由连接 OpenAI Responses 上游时，回复已经结束又收到一条错误（#8138）。上游在发完结束事件后才断开连接时，CC Switch 会在正常结束之后再补发一个流错误，客户端可能把完整的回复当成失败。现在结束之后的连接错误不再转发。
  • 在 MCP、提示词、定价编辑页再次点击侧栏的当前页面，会丢掉「有未保存的修改」的保护（#7900）。以前会弹出放弃更改的确认，确认后编辑页还开着，但之后离开页面就不再提醒，改动直接丢失。现在点击当前页面什么也不做。
  • 上游返回空错误内容时，错误信息结尾是一个孤零零的冒号（#6910），看起来像 CC Switch 吞掉了上游的说明。现在会写明「上游未返回错误内容」。
  • Pi 会话历史的速度一直显示「—」（#8278）。Pi 会话导入时耗时固定记成 0，现在按每条回复的请求开始时间和写入时间估算耗时，符合条件的记录能显示估算速度。
  • Pi 配置目录指向 WSL 时，每次写入都先尝试 WSL 不支持的系统调用（#8245）。启用 Skills、保存 AGENTS.md 时，现在直接使用在 WSL 路径上可用的写法；自动模式下同步 Skills 改为复制，手动选了软链接的仍会明确报错。
  • Windows 上自动更新重启后，读取 OpenCode、Codex 自带的模型列表时仍可能找不到工具（#8262）。v4.0.11 修好了安装和升级，这次把补全后的 PATH 也用到这两处；PATH 里 %PNPM_HOME% 这样的变量引用也会按用户和系统环境变量展开。

升级说明

数据库结构没有变化，仍然是 20。

  • 没打开用量页时，后台每 10 分钟扫描一次会话日志（以前是 60 秒），所以不经路由的用量最多晚 10 分钟进入统计；打开用量页会立即扫描。以前选了「关闭」的，改为 60 秒。
  • Pi 的速度估算只对升级后新导入的记录生效，已经导入的记录仍显示「—」。
  • 新预设和 StreamLake 的改动只影响新添加的供应商，已添加的 StreamLake 供应商仍使用原来的接入点 ID。
  • Claude Desktop 选择本地预设时，表单会清空 Key，需要手动填上占位值：Ollama 填 ollama，LM Studio 填 lmstudio，llama.cpp 填 no-key，vLLM 填 dummy；启动服务时设了 Key 的，填你设的值。

致谢

感谢 @funkpopo（用量页会话扫描间隔、「应用」页列对齐、卸载反馈与 Windows PATH 跟进、Pi 会话速度估算）、@xue-ding-e（Skills 恢复失败、Responses 回复结束后的流错误、搜索框遮挡卡片）、@alkank（土耳其语界面与托盘）、@AristurtleHu（Pi 配置目录在 WSL 时的写入）、@Owlbay（未保存更改保护）、@loulanyue（上游空错误内容）、@xwil1（OpenCode 硅基流动预设）、@Lingbou（Windows CI 测试超时）的贡献。

感谢 @HessianZ、@bubblevv、@JimMoen、@chufeng、@Wikian233 报告问题、提供复现信息和修复思路。

完整的技术细节请见 CHANGELOG。

v4.0.11CC Switch v4.0.11 Stable2026-10-13T15:35:29Z

CC Switch v4.0.11

本版本为应用增加了卸载功能，现在你可以在「应用」页安装、更新、卸载编程应用了。卸载前确认框会列出要卸载的安装路径和将执行的命令，只卸载能确认来源的安装，配置和会话数据都保留；菜单里另有按安装方式列出的手动卸载命令。添加套餐类供应商（Kimi、智谱、MiniMax 等）时，所有应用都会默认开启套餐额度查询，不再只限 Claude Code，一键导入链接也一样。Codex 用量统计修了两个问题：分叉出来的会话不再每分钟整份重读一遍，Codex 桌面版长会话翻页后分出的子代理用量也能统计了。另外修复了 Windows 上 CC Switch 自动更新重启后安装、升级工具找不到 npm，全屏页面打开时闪烁，以及 Skills 和 MCP 批量开关全部失败时仍提示成功等问题。4.0 完整的更新内容请见 v4.0 发布说明。

English → | 日本語版 →

新增

「应用」页可以卸载已安装的工具。 每个已安装的工具旁边多了「卸载」按钮。点击后先检查当前使用的是哪一处安装，确认框里列出将卸载的安装路径和将执行的命令，确认后才执行；执行前会重新检查一遍，安装情况有变化就不执行。只卸载能确认来源的安装：

  • 用 npm 全局安装的（核对包名和安装前缀）；
  • 用 Homebrew 安装的 Claude Code、Codex（cask）和 Gemini CLI、OpenCode（formula），其他 Homebrew 包一律不碰；
  • 用官方安装脚本安装的 Claude Code、Codex、Grok Build、OpenCode，只删除当前使用的那个可执行文件和它的启动入口，不删除目录，Claude Code 的其他旧版本仍会保留；
  • Hermes 调用它自己的 hermes uninstall --yes；OpenClaw 先执行官方命令卸载 Gateway 服务，成功后再卸载 CLI。
  • Windows 上装在 WSL 里的工具会在对应发行版内卸载，和版本检测用同一个 shell，所以用 nvm 之类装的工具也能找到。

配置和会话数据都保留；有多处安装时只卸载当前使用的那一处，其他的保留。认不出来源的安装不会自动卸载，会提示改用手动命令。「应用」页菜单里新增「手动卸载命令」，按安装方式（npm、pnpm、Bun、Homebrew 等）和运行环境列出各工具的卸载命令，可以复制到终端执行。

预设： 新增 StreamLake 国内站、AIComing、SoruxGPT Coding Plan、GrooRoute Pro；修正 StreamLake 国际站 OpenCode、OpenClaw、Pi 预设的地址；AIGoCode 改用新域名。

改进

套餐额度查询在所有应用里默认开启。 添加地址属于套餐的供应商（Kimi、智谱、MiniMax、火山引擎、OpenCode Go、Command Code）时，以前只有 Claude Code 默认开启套餐额度查询，Codex、OpenCode、Pi、Hermes 等应用要再手动打开一次。现在所有应用都默认开启。一键导入链接也一样：链接没带用量查询脚本、地址属于套餐时，导入后默认开启，确认框里会写明「套餐额度：Kimi For Coding」这样的套餐名；链接写了 usageEnabled=false 时仍然关闭。ZenMux 仍只在 Claude Code 里默认开启。已经添加的供应商不受影响。

全屏页面不再淡入淡出。 v4.0.10 去掉了添加、编辑供应商页面的淡入淡出，但「新增定价」「配置用量查询」「请求地址管理」、统一供应商、工作区文件等页面在 Windows 上打开时仍会闪一下。现在所有全屏页面都不再淡入淡出，macOS 和 Linux 上也一样；授权中心从右侧滑入的动画保留。工作区文件和每日记忆会在内容读完后再打开编辑页，不再先闪出加载页。

修复

  • Windows 上 CC Switch 自动更新重启后，安装、升级工具时找不到 npm（#8171）。安装包更新后自动重启的 CC Switch 拿不到完整的用户 PATH。版本检测以前已经从注册表补回了 PATH，所以显示工具已安装；但执行安装、升级命令时用的仍是残缺的 PATH，于是找不到 npm。现在执行命令时也用补全后的 PATH，卸载同样受益。
  • Codex 分叉出来的会话每分钟被整份重读一遍（#8217、#8209）。子代理会话、「分支到新聊天」或回退产生的分叉会话，如果父会话还没写到分叉时刻，用量导入会先跳过、稍后再试。从 v3.18 起，每 60 秒一次的同步都会把这些会话整份重新读一遍，再在日志里写一条警告；分叉会话多的用户累计读盘可达数 TB，日志很快涨到轮转上限。现在会记下子会话和父会话文件的状态，文件没变就直接跳过，有变化才重试。计费规则不变：父会话一直没写到分叉时刻（例如闲置很久之后才分叉）的会话，用量仍不计入，这个口径还在讨论中（#5687）。
  • Codex 桌面版长会话翻页后，分出的子代理用量全部丢失（#7084、#8210）。Codex 桌面版把长会话分页保存，第一页写满后续写到新文件，CC Switch 以前只找得到第一页，翻页之后分出的子代理就一直被判为「父会话还没写到分叉时刻」，用量从 v3.20.1 起一直没统计。现在会把同一会话的各页按顺序拼起来再判断。这些子代理会话以前没被导入过，升级后下一轮同步会自动补上，只要会话文件还在。分叉线程自己翻页之后的续写页仍然统计不到，会在后续版本处理（#8161）。
  • Skills 和 MCP 批量启用、停用全部失败时仍提示成功。 现在全部失败时显示红色错误提示，部分失败时显示警告；撤销也按实际结果提示，不再总是显示「已撤销」。
  • 工作区编辑器可能把上一个文件的内容存进当前文件。 打开一个文件再关掉、接着打开另一个文件时，如果后者读取失败，编辑页会在新文件名下继续显示前一个文件的内容，这时点保存就会覆盖新文件。现在读取失败会回到文件列表。

升级说明

数据库结构没有变化，仍然是 20。

  • Codex 子代理用量的补导在升级后的下一轮同步中自动进行，只涉及以前因为父会话分页而一直跳过的子会话，不会重复计入已经导入过的用量。
  • 套餐额度默认开启只影响新添加和新导入的供应商。已有的供应商要开启，在卡片上点「配置用量查询」选择 Token Plan 模板即可。

致谢

感谢 @funkpopo（「应用」页卸载、Windows 自动更新后找不到 npm）、@NestorRay（全屏页面闪烁、工作区编辑器读取）、@Carl723000（Codex 分叉会话每分钟重读）、@Lingbou（Codex 分页会话拼接，并报告了重读告警刷屏和分页索引缺失）、@jgwy（最早报告并实现了分页会话拼接）、@xue-ding-e（Skills 批量开关失败提示）、@mozzie49（批量撤销结果提示）的贡献。

感谢 @andyyxw、@syanle、@a790798959-hub、@imsquner、@loulanyue、@YiMore7 报告问题、提供复现信息和修复思路。

完整的技术细节请见 CHANGELOG。

v0.8.157 Stable2026-10-14T09:47:17Z

HyperFrames v0.8.157

Released on 2026-10-14.

Renders inside WSL use the graphics card again on Chrome 150 and later. Before, they fell back to slow software rendering.

Fixes

  • Engine: Renders under WSL reach the GPU on Chrome 150 and later (38cfcdd56cd, #5591). Chrome 150 and later find no GPU while WSLg's WAYLAND_DISPLAY is set, so HyperFrames checks again without it and renders that way only when that reaches the GPU.
  • Core: Keep the colour grade on a graded video's first rendered frame (95837a3df7, #5575). This shipped in v0.8.156 but was missing from its notes.

Internal

  • Studio: Settle the edit accuracy bench on frames and network, and reuse the producer Chrome (d9edffa3a4e, #5571).
  • Docker: Pin FFmpeg 8.0.1 in the Docker images and host CI (692b89b6e50, #5576).

Full changelog

https://github.com/heygen-com/hyperframes/compare/v0.8.156...v0.8.157

v0.8.156 Stable2026-10-14T04:37:43Z

HyperFrames v0.8.156

Released on 2026-10-14.

Videos from screen recordings no longer freeze partway through on FFmpeg 8. Segmented renders with several workers start faster when media is slow to load, and long audio mixes do less work. @hyperframes/inspector no longer ships agent tools, so harnesses define their own.

Breaking Changes

  • Inspector: Stop shipping agent tools. Harnesses now define their own (abe97f6e4c, #5570). inspect_components, query_timeline, the stdio MCP server and the componentsTool, timelineTool, toolContent and InspectionTool exports from @hyperframes/inspector/live are gone. The HTTP contract (POST /inspect, /timeline, /element-map) is unchanged, and InspectRequest and TimelineRequest are now exported so a harness can build its own schema.

Fixes

  • Engine: Renders under WSL use the GPU through full Chrome when the headless shell cannot (9b996059af, #5574). It used to fall back to software rendering.
  • Producer: Start segmented render workers together so slow media waits overlap (d77b03c24c, #5573). Each worker used to wait for the one before it to finish loading media.
  • Engine: Sample VFR video by timestamp so it does not freeze on FFmpeg 8 (6afcb48395, #5572). FFmpeg 8 honours frame durations, so screen recordings with long, overlapping frames held one frame and dropped the rest. Cached frames from older versions are re-extracted.
  • Engine: Avoid padding every audio mix input (3de4763d53, #5563). Short clips no longer stay in the mix for the whole composition, so audio work no longer grows with clip count times length.

Internal

  • Studio: Run the edit accuracy slice on PRs and the full grid in the merge queue (9a2c1c6e80, #5566).

Full changelog

https://github.com/heygen-com/hyperframes/compare/v0.8.155...v0.8.156

v0.8.155 Stable2026-10-13T23:33:42Z

HyperFrames v0.8.155

Released on 2026-10-13.

MP4 renders at widths like 1000, 1080 or 1112 no longer get a black strip down the right edge on newer FFmpeg. Both the Studio render route and the producer render server now take encoder settings such as CRF, and distributed renders keep the CRF you ask for.

Features

  • Producer: The render server's /render and /render/stream accept crf, videoBitrate and videoFrameFormat (5de2712ad5, #5550). A bad videoFrameFormat now returns a 400 instead of being ignored.
  • Studio Server: Accept crf, videoBitrate, videoFrameFormat and hdrMode on the render route (ab8a4da53f, #5549). Out-of-range values return a 400, and crf with videoBitrate together is rejected.

Fixes

  • Engine: Keep the rightmost 8 columns of SDR MP4 at widths like 1080 (401354cd8f, #5559). FFmpeg 7.1 and 8.0 dropped the last 8 pixel columns to black when the width was 8 more than a multiple of 16.
  • Producer: Keep the requested CRF in the distributed constant-frame-rate re-encode (bf4cf860fb, #5557). It used to fall back to the quality preset.

Full changelog

https://github.com/heygen-com/hyperframes/compare/v0.8.154...v0.8.155

astral-sh/uv ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
0.13.1 Stable2026-10-14T05:15:30Z

Release Notes

Released on 2026-10-14.

Enhancements

  • Ignore Requires-Python upper bounds in uv pip check and uv pip install --strict, consistent with resolution (#22500)
  • Support Linux SPARC by falling back when filesystem cloning and detailed cache accounting are unavailable (#22463)
  • Report missing trusted hashes in resolution errors (#22241)

Python

  • Add CPython 3.16.0a1 (#22527)

Preview features

  • Allow uv tool upgrade to upgrade installed tools when tool-install-locks is enabled (#22498)
  • Add preliminary support for parsing PEP 825 variant labels in wheel filenames with wheel-variants (#22039)

Performance

  • Reduce uv's binary size (#22429, #22430, #22432)
  • Speed up Simple API parsing (#22429)
  • Reduce package metadata parsing overhead (#22239)

Bug fixes

  • Prevent tool and managed Python uninstalls from deleting unrelated files beside their storage directories (#22495)
  • Restore support for source distributions containing hard links or full-length tar user or group names (#22516, #22522)
  • Preserve device files and FIFOs used as uv pip compile or uv export output destinations (#22496)
  • Fix sys._base_executable detection for environments created from symlinked interpreters (#22520)
  • Prevent concurrent readers from observing partially written lockfiles, Python version files, or managed Python sysconfig files (#22399, #22402, #22403)
  • Serialize concurrent project commands that reach the same environment through real and symlinked paths (#22390)
  • Prevent installs, syncs, and environment creation from failing when they race with uv cache clean (#22387)
  • Prevent concurrent tool installations from corrupting an environment when the last tool is uninstalled (#22391)
  • Persist changed upgrade settings even when uv tool upgrade leaves the tool version unchanged (#22499)
  • Reuse environments with installed Git dependencies pinned to full commit hashes without resolving again (#22464)

Documentation

  • Explain how to select an emulated Python interpreter on Windows ARM64 when native wheels are unavailable (#22424)
  • Fix the .netrc format documentation link (#22514)

Other changes

  • Publish Python 3.15-based uv Docker images under stable tags (#22503)

Install uv 0.13.1

Install prebuilt binaries via shell script

curl --proto '=https' --tlsv1.2 -LsSf https://releases.astral.sh/github/uv/releases/download/0.13.1/uv-installer.sh | sh

Install prebuilt binaries via powershell script

powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -c "irm https://releases.astral.sh/github/uv/releases/download/0.13.1/uv-installer.ps1 | iex"

Verifying GitHub Artifact Attestations

The artifacts in this release have attestations generated with GitHub Artifact Attestations. These can be verified by using the GitHub CLI:

gh attestation verify <file-path of downloaded artifact> --repo astral-sh/uv

You can also download the attestation from GitHub and verify against that directly:

gh attestation verify <file-path of downloaded artifact> --bundle <file-path of downloaded attestation>

unixwin/niubash ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.4.0Niubash v1.4.0 Stable2026-10-14T04:40:41Z

Niubash v1.4.0 packages for Windows, Linux, and macOS.

Engine: rubash git c85c3ed0bfca — every platform build in this release compiled that same revision. Verify locally with niu --version, which prints the same revision.

Windows (x64, ARM64): Inno Setup installers (*-setup.exe) add user PATH and Windows Terminal profile integration; portable zips bundle niu with WinuxCmd and bash/sh shims.

Windows packages come with the must-install bundle pre-installed (niubash#189, niubash#209): niugit (native Windows git — the full plugin distro's five entries install through git, so a fresh machine without git.exe no longer fails niu setup), gawk 5.4.1 with plain awk working too, plus ripgrep (rg) and fd — all landing in the bundled WinuxCmd root as wpm JSON-package payloads, assembled at CI time with no runtime downloads, no archive formats beyond the release zip itself, and no symlinks. Pre-install is best-effort, not a promise: on legs where the package index has no matching artifact (currently niugit and gawk on the ARM64 builds; ripgrep and fd have ARM64 artifacts) the package ships WITHOUT that entry — run winuxcmd wpm install <name> once after installing to add it. The pre-install list and its recorded exclusions live in scripts/release/preinstall.json.

niubash-skill-v<version>.zip is the AI agent skill bundle (SKILL.md + references, WinuxCmd-skill layout): unpack it into an agent skills directory, or let the shell do it — niu skill install embeds the same content.

Linux (x86_64, aarch64; glibc): portable tarballs with the niu binary, README, and LICENSE — Unix builds bundle nothing, native system tools are used. Untar anywhere and run ./niu.

macOS (aarch64, x86_64): portable tarballs with the niu binary, README, and LICENSE. Untar anywhere and run ./niu.

Every artifact was smoke-verified on the build OS of the job that produced it, before upload: niu -c 'echo ok', printf 'a\nb\n' | niu -c 'sort | head -1', an offline NIU_PLUGIN_SPEC=<empty> niu plugin sync --bootstrap (exit 0, no output, no network), and a niu --version check against this tag and the engine revision.

Integrity (niubash#311): SHA256SUMS lists the SHA-256 of every shipped artifact, and each Windows installer has a .sha256 sidecar beside it. niu --self-update verifies the installer against the sidecar before running it and aborts rather than execute anything it cannot verify. The same digests feed the winget manifest InstallerSha256.

tw93/Pake ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
V3.17.5V3.17.5 Compass Stable2026-10-14T04:01:46Z

<div align="center"> <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/tw93/Pake/main/assets/logo.png" alt="Pake Logo" width="120" height="120" style="border-radius:50%" /> <h1 style="margin: 12px 0 6px;">Pake V3.17.5</h1> <p><em>Turn any webpage into a desktop app with one command.</em></p> </div>

Changelog

  1. New --no-restore-last-url ("restoreLastUrl": false in a config file) opens the packaged URL on every launch while keeping cookies, local storage, and logins. Without it, apps still reopen the last page.
  2. The Pake GitHub Action now moves the package the CLI actually built on that runner into the output directory, instead of picking a build-script .exe on Windows or finding nothing elsewhere, and uses: tw93/Pake@v3 now follows the newest release on its own.
  3. The fork build workflow passes its form inputs to the CLI safely, so URLs and names containing $ package correctly, and Linux packages upload even when the app name has capital letters.
  4. The DeepSeek app has a new icon built from DeepSeek's official whale.
  5. The image dependency sharp is updated to 0.35.5 for the libheif security advisories.
  6. The README is now also available in Traditional Chinese, Japanese, Korean, German, and French.

更新日志

  1. 新增 --no-restore-last-url，配置文件里写 "restoreLastUrl": false，每次启动都会打开打包时的网址，Cookie、本地存储和登录状态都会保留，不加时仍然回到上次打开的页面。
  2. Pake GitHub Action 会把 CLI 在当前运行器上真正打出的安装包放进输出目录，Windows 上不再误拿构建脚本生成的 .exe，其他平台也不会再找不到包，uses: tw93/Pake@v3 以后也会自动跟上最新版本。
  3. Fork 里的打包工作流改为安全地传递表单输入，网址或名称里带 $ 也能正常打包，应用名带大写字母时 Linux 安装包也能正常上传。
  4. DeepSeek 应用换成了用官方鲸鱼标志重做的新图标。
  5. 图片处理依赖 sharp 升级到 0.35.5，用来应对 libheif 的安全公告。
  6. README 新增繁体中文、日语、韩语、德语和法语版本。

Special thanks to @leo0570 for the request and to @jakero for the pull request behind this release. If Pake helps you, please consider giving it a star and recommending it to your friends.

https://github.com/tw93/Pake

NousResearch/hermes-agent ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.22.0Hermes Agent v0.22.0 — The Threshold Release Stable2026-10-14T03:21:46Z

Hermes Agent v0.22.0

Release Date: October 13, 2026 Since v0.21.0: ~24,200 commits · ~6,080 merged PRs · ~15,600 files changed · ~2,045,000 insertions · ~1,140,000 deletions · ~7,180 issues closed · ~2,520 contributors

The Threshold Release. Hermes was the god of thresholds: the doorway, the crossing, the boundary stone. v0.21.0 assembled the pantheon; v0.22.0 is about getting through the door. A fresh desktop install now opens on a guided setup chat that runs on a free trial model, so a newcomer is talking to Hermes before they own an API key. Connectors carry the agent across into the apps you already use, with no account needed to start. Local models bring it home to your own hardware with a one-click llama.cpp runtime. And the threshold itself got sturdier: tested SemVer stable releases that installs follow by default, signed desktop packages (including the Microsoft Store), and one package manager underneath everything. Around that spine: a 600-plugin catalog, the Claude 5.5 / GPT-6 model wave, password-manager sign-in for the browser, a bot screen you can take over, one backend and one gateway for every profile, and a codebase a third smaller. This is the largest window Hermes has shipped, about 2.5x the v0.21.0 cycle, and it rolls up and fully documents the v0.21.1–v0.21.7 patch releases.

<!-- HERMES_BUILDS_TABLE -->

✨ Highlights

  • A first launch that sets itself up — Open the desktop app on a computer where you have never used Hermes and it starts a short setup chat instead of a settings screen. One card at a time it asks your name, an accent color and a layout, which apps to connect, which plugins to install, then gives a quick tour and offers up to four first tasks picked for you. It runs on a free trial model (nous/welcome, once per OS user), so it needs no key; Skip setup ends it and Settings → Run onboarding again brings it back. The agent also gained set_theme, so "make it violet, dark mode" works in any desktop chat. (#137429, #137522, #137807, #134209, #105258 — @alt-glitch, @OutThisLife, @ethernet8023, @rewbs)

  • Connectors: Hermes works inside your apps — The agent can now act in hosted apps such as Linear and Notion through the Nous tool gateway: ask for an app, sign in once in your browser, and the tools are usable in the same turn. You do not need a Nous account to start; the first time you use an app, Hermes creates a free connectors-only identity. The desktop has a new Connectors page that puts hosted apps and your MCP servers in one list (it replaces the MCP tab), the same setup card appears in the TUI and CLI, and you can connect several named accounts of one app (a work and a home account) and manage them with /connectors or hermes connectors. (#106842, #111008, #119074, #135143, #135072, #135097, #135151, #135173, #137426 — @alt-glitch, @rewbs)

  • Local models in one click — Hermes can now run open models entirely on your machine with nothing leaving it: no account, no API key. It installs and maintains llama.cpp with the right engine for your hardware (CPU, CUDA, Metal, Vulkan or ROCm), rates every catalog model against your GPU and RAM, and picks the highest-quality build that fits. Downloads pause and resume, the server starts and stops with Hermes, and engine updates are one click. Start from Settings → Providers → Local Models on Windows, macOS and Linux, or hermes model → Local models in a terminal. The catalog includes Qwen3.8 27B, Qwen3.8 Flash Next (with its MTP speculative head), Qwen3.6 35B-A3B and DeepSeek V4 Flash, plus any GGUF from Hugging Face. (#100667, #101823, #128768, #111423, #133111, #134116 — @jquesnelle, @ethernet8023)

  • Stable release channels — Hermes now ships tested SemVer releases. Every release passes source CI, Docker, Nix, signed desktop builds and install/upgrade runs on every OS before it is published, and source installs now move from release to release by default instead of tracking every commit on main. Prefer the bleeding edge? hermes update --set-channel main, or the new channel switch in Desktop Settings → About. Moving to an older release is labelled a rollback and never happens without asking, and installer reruns keep you on the channel you chose. (#102765, #135847, #135949, #136313, #137780 — @ethernet8023, @KoNit-K)

  • Signed desktop packages, the Microsoft Store, and one package manager — The desktop app is now a sealed, signed package that carries its own Python and Node and updates itself, so first launch builds nothing. Windows gets a signed MSIX (x64 and ARM64) through App Installer or the Microsoft Store; macOS gets a signed, notarized build for Apple Silicon. Underneath, a new unified package manager (hermes pm) owns every pinned, hash-checked tool and Python environment: updates build a new environment and switch only once it passes its check, so a failed or killed update can no longer leave you half-applied. (#102765, #132361, #132386, #120339, #122221 — @ethernet8023)

  • The Plugin Catalog: 600 reviewed plugins — The plugin catalog launched this cycle and grew to 600 entries, each pinned to a reviewed commit and installable from Hermes Desktop or the CLI. Browse by category and star ranking with a page per plugin and per author; catalog rules keep listed plugins from patching Hermes internals or shipping unscannable binaries. Memory providers now live with their makers (Honcho, Supermemory, Hindsight, Mem0, OpenViking, Holographic), as do Home Assistant, Spotify and Langfuse: existing users are migrated automatically, and the default install got leaner. (#69446, #111415, #117808, #113381, #114732, #131267, #132476, #136436 — @itpartypattaya, @happy5318, and 400+ plugin authors)

  • The Claude 5.5 and GPT-6 model wave — Claude Fable 5.1, Opus 5.5, Sonnet 5.5 and Haiku 5.5, GPT-6 Astra, Sol and Luna plus GPT-6.1 Sol, and Gemini 4 Argon are pickable on Nous Portal, OpenRouter and native routes where offered. Prior reasoning is now replayed by default on every provider that can read it, across resume, branches and compaction, so long multi-step work keeps its train of thought. And when a provider has a bad hour, Hermes waits out transient 503/529/timeouts in visible, bounded cycles instead of ending your turn. (#100577, #119304, #126842, #134759, #128248, #137955, #136358, #116353 — @kshitijk4poor, @unsupportedpastels, @100yenadmin, @mssteuer, @xxxigm)

  • Sign in to websites with your password manager — The browser agent can log into sites with logins from 1Password, Bitwarden or Hermes's own encrypted vault, and the model never sees the password. If nothing is saved, you get a masked prompt in the CLI, TUI or Desktop to save it, one-time codes are filled without entering the conversation, and card and address fills at checkout need your confirmation. (#106480, #107585, #137084)

  • Bot Screen: watch your bot work, take over when it needs you — A bot running on a Linux gateway can have its own desktop that Hermes Desktop streams live. When it hits a login, a 2FA prompt or a CAPTCHA, you take the screen, finish the step yourself, and hand control back while the bot carries on with your session. With a Docker, SSH or Singularity backend, the bot's screen, computer use and the browser run inside the sandbox, never on your host. (#108914, #121169, #121110)

  • One backend and one gateway for every profile — Hermes now runs a single messaging gateway and a single desktop backend per machine that serve all of your profiles, instead of one process per profile. Creating a profile makes its bots live without a restart, each profile keeps its own credentials, allowlists, cron jobs and sessions, and hermes update migrates existing per-profile setups automatically. Fewer processes, no token clashes, one thing to start and stop. (#112854, #108928, #118273, #117983, #118246, #118027)

  • See and steer your subagents live — Running subagents now stay visible above the composer in the CLI (F6), TUI (Ctrl+T) and Desktop with each child's task, elapsed time and live transcript, plus Steer and Stop buttons. /stop reaches background children and hands back their partial work, and delegate_task can pass screenshots and mocks to vision-capable children. (#105593, #115616, #97689)

  • A model picker that knows your plans — Star your favorite models, see a subscription running low ("17% left · resets 3:00 PM") and when a rate limit lifts right in the picker, and pick reasoning effort as part of choosing a model in hermes model, /model (/model X --reasoning high) and Desktop. You switch before you hit the wall instead of after. (#126535, #133392, #133408, #110346, #113117 — @OutThisLife, @anotherchu)

  • Hermes in your language, everywhere — Every user-facing string in the CLI, messaging gateway, TUI and Desktop now goes through one translation catalog with full coverage in all 16 bundled languages. New languages can ship as plugins, and any profile can override individual strings with a small YAML file. (#126296)

  • Leaner in every sense — The whole codebase was simplified with zero behavior change: source went from about 1.06M to 698K lines (−34%) and gateway/run.py shrank from 34,847 lines to 5,512. Prompts got lighter too: about 19 rarely used tools now load only when the agent searches for them, roughly halving the desktop tool schema sent on every call, and another ~2.1k tokens came off the fixed prompt. Local models prefill less on every turn. (#102117, #97979, #106676, #137084 — @alt-glitch)

  • Small comforts that add up — Opt-in vim keys in the CLI prompt, a git-branch field in the status bar, a bell and desktop notification when Hermes is waiting on you, Ctrl+C that clears your draft instead of killing the turn, an editable /queue, and hermes --native to let your own terminal handle TUI scrollback and search. On Desktop, a new Simple mode turns the app into a plain chat window, and errors now say what went wrong with a one-click fix. (#106412, #106434, #101284, #101320, #137065, #95999, #121035, #118587, #111601 — @saforem2, @TurgutKural, @glitchbunny0, @harshmoney123, @pierrenode, @abhinav11082001-stack, @OutThisLife)

⚠️ Upgrade notes

  • Python 3.14 is the runtime for managed installs (the package manager installs it for you). KittenTTS is unavailable on 3.14. (#102765)
  • Windows desktop is MSIX-only and needs Windows 11 22H2 or newer; the NSIS/MSI installers are gone. The macOS app is Apple Silicon only in default builds. (#102765, #136603)
  • One gateway per machine is the default. hermes update folds per-profile gateways into the multiplexed default when nothing conflicts. (#112854)
  • Chats no longer reset on an idle/daily timer. session_reset idle/daily modes are retired (configs that still set them log a warning); /new or /reset starts fresh. (#105021, #126244)
  • Old sessions are pruned automatically. sessions.auto_prune now defaults to on: ended sessions inactive for 90 days are deleted at startup. Pinned and open sessions are never touched; set sessions.auto_prune: false to keep everything.
  • Memory providers moved to catalog plugins (Honcho, Supermemory, Hindsight, Mem0, OpenViking, Holographic), as did Home Assistant, Spotify and Langfuse. Existing users are migrated on update or first start, with data intact. ByteRover and RetainDB remain bundled.
  • Plugin authors: the old plugin compatibility import layer is gone (#126164), hermes sync (Skill Sync) was removed (#132465), and the bundled tirith scanner was removed (#133832). Several tools were renamed (todo → todo_list, cronjob → cronjob_manage, process → process_manage); the old names still work. (#97979)

🚪 Onboarding, Free Tier, Connectors & Local Models

First-run onboarding

  • Guided setup chat on a fresh desktop install (see Highlights). Existing installs and remote (SSH, URL, Cloud) backends don't get it automatically; the old setup-profile flow and /initiate-setup are deleted. (#137420, #134215, #108292 — @alt-glitch, @ethernet8023, @OutThisLife)
  • The agent can restyle the app — The new set_theme tool sets the accent (a swatch or any #rrggbb), light/dark/system mode and layout, or shows a picker; "make it violet" works in any desktop chat. (#137522 — @alt-glitch)

Nous free tier

  • A free model for your first desktop launch — A first desktop install with no provider or Nous account gets the free nous/welcome model as a one-time trial (once per OS user), so onboarding needs no key. The CLI, TUI and gateway get no account or free model. HERMES_GUEST_ONBOARDING is gone. (#137807, #105258, #105260 — @alt-glitch)
  • Sign in and keep your apps — hermes auth upgrade, or /login in any chat or messaging DM, upgrades the free identity to a full Nous account with your connected apps. (#105259, #105261 — @alt-glitch)
  • Clear limits — The free rate limit ends the turn at once with the reset time; every free-tier failure has a code and a plain sentence; the app handles the account service's browser check; free-tier users see a Terms and Privacy notice. Nous accounts without credits can pick models billed to a subscription they hold and get free managed fast web search. (#134049, #111970, #133824, #117079 — @alt-glitch, @kshitijk4poor, @rewbs)

Connectors

  • Hosted app connectors, the Connectors page and several accounts per app (see Highlights). Connector tools can also send your local files as attachments and save the files they return. nous.guest: false turns guest identities off; tools.connectors.enabled: false turns connectors off. (#111238, #138014 — @alt-glitch)
  • One connect flow everywhere — Hosted apps and local MCP servers share one setup card (desktop dialog, TUI callout or CLI panel), and the agent can use new tools in the same turn. On desktop, manage_connections can show a browsable app directory. (#111008, #109517, #137426 — @alt-glitch)

Local models

  • One-click local models (see Highlights). Also: Hugging Face GGUF downloads with pause/resume, and a one-time setup offer after onboarding on machines that can run the recommended model. (#129506 — @alt-glitch)
  • Engine updates and wider GPU support — An Update now prompt updates the engine in one click. NVIDIA on Linux gets CUDA, AMD and Intel GPUs get Vulkan sized by their own memory, and every backend runs llama.cpp b11370. (#111423, #133111, #131782 — @jquesnelle, @skyecon, @mbeattietex1-star, @Belaquaa)
  • Better recommendations — Only models that fit fully in GPU memory are recommended, and the 27B no longer on hardware that runs it poorly. Qwen3.8 Flash Next with its MTP head fits 128 GB machines; NVIDIA Spark recommendations are tuned. (#108429, #135438, #134116, #112107 — @jquesnelle, @RyanUnderhill)
  • Your own local servers — LM Studio has a provider profile, Ollama pickers no longer flicker, a self-started llama.cpp server shows up in /model, and fallback to the desktop-managed model works. (#134320, #113129, #116679, #131795 — @kshitijk4poor, @DaxLee9988, @fangliquanflq, @Finn763, @albeight)

📦 Stable Releases, Desktop Packages & Package Manager

Stable releases & update channels

  • Tested SemVer stable releases and the channel switch (see Highlights). v0.21.6 and v0.21.7 were the first through the new gate; forks stay on main, and an install ahead of the newest release waits for the next.
  • Lighter update checks — one GitHub API call a day replaces git fetch every 30 minutes. (#107648)

Desktop bundles & the Microsoft Store

  • Sealed, signed desktop packages (see Highlights). In a package, hermes update points to the right updater (App Installer, Store or macOS). (#134911)
  • Packaged installs work end to end — the agent's terminal runs hermes, Uninstall explains OS removal, and Python-dependency plugins install inside MSIX. (#134051, #135333 — @alt-glitch)

Unified package manager

  • hermes pm (see Highlights): pinned, hash-checked Python 3.14, uv, Node, ripgrep, FFmpeg, Chromium and Git/Bash on Windows, with hermes pm doctor|status|repair|gc. Contributors use source ./activate (or .ps1/.fish).
  • Dependency environments are generations — new ones are checked before use, so a failed install keeps the working one; updates and hermes pm gc reclaim old ones (92 GB to 18 GB on one host). (#124968, #133808 — @JoaoMarcos44)
  • Optional features install on first use — opt-in SDKs (Anthropic, Bedrock) load mid-session without a restart; security.allow_lazy_installs: false turns this off. (#122221 — @ethernet8023)
  • Plugins declare Python dependencies — resolved with core across profiles; conflicts are refused, and updates disable a plugin that no longer fits. Hindsight, Honcho, Supermemory, Mem0 and Home Assistant moved to the catalog and auto-install where used. (#113851, #122103, #114569, #132469 — @ethernet8023)

Install & update

  • Updates can't half-apply (see Highlights). A killed update repairs itself on the next launch. (#132361, #120339)
  • Faster updates, smaller checkouts — updates rebuild only what changed (16.5 s vs 32.5 s on macOS), clones are blobless, and partial-clone .git growth is fixed. (#135092, #133016, #131679 — @kshitijk4poor, @ethernet8023, @liuhao1024, @whyyagswhy)
  • Small, hash-checked installers — install.sh/install.ps1 verify pinned uv and Git, hand off to PM, log to logs/install.log, and now work on musl Linux (Alpine, Void), Windows 10 and Unicode Windows paths. (#124964, #125598, #132430 — @JoaoMarcos44, @kshitijk4poor, @Finn763, @OutThisLife)
  • Failures say why — with opt-in telemetry, install, update, skill and plugin failures record a reason. (#133809)

Docker / Nix / Termux / Windows

  • Docker — pinned Debian 13 base with full Chromium; stable/latest move only on a published release; baked-in extras return after a restart. (#135726, #135084 — @unsupportedpastels)
  • Nix, Termux, Windows — Nix shares the pins; its desktop app gets Wayland; Termux moves to a signed APT package (pkg upgrade hermes-agent), though its docs still warn it is broken; Windows updates finish while the old runtime is in use. (#128144, #122146, #131779 — @ethernet8023, @janviernine)

CI & release engineering

  • Release gates — E2E suites run on release builds, the full suite on native Windows, and SSH/Docker backends against real daemons. (#135409, #96458, #136602 — @ethernet8023)

🧠 Providers & Models

New providers

  • Providers as plugins — ACP and CLI-driven providers install out of tree via hermes plugins install; --provider chatgpt picks ChatGPT Codex OAuth. (#101456, #115761 — @AlexanderPrendota, @DarRahman)
  • Provider upgrades — Bedrock serves GPT-6 (1M) and enforces Claude Guardrails; Azure Foundry lists deployments; Gemini takes full JSON Schema tools; OpenCode Go routes Claude via Messages. (#133573, #107815, #115770, #98928, #134848 — @victor-kyriazakos, @JoaoMarcos44, @xxxigm, @thejpanganiban, @pandacooming)
  • New knobs — agent.text_verbosity sets Responses answer length; named custom providers run on the Codex app-server. (#115880, #115905 — @Beandon13, @cosin2077)

Model catalog

  • Claude 5.x — Opus 5.5 also on native Anthropic and Bedrock (1M). (#122633 — @kshitijk4poor, @didodim)
  • GPT-6 — Astra/Astra Pro in standard, fast and flex; opt-in Codex -900k; /fast ultrafast for Astra and 6.1 Sol, per plan. (#103261, #119410, #136950 — @daniil-grois, @sdalonzo)
  • Gemini 3.8 Flash and Gemini 4 Argon. (#101450, #137989 — @kshitijk4poor, @dgkaaks)
  • More — DeepSeek V4.1 Flash, Qwen3.8-Max-0902, Grok 4.7, GLM-5.3-FlashX, Muse Spark 1.3, Solar Pro 4/Mini 4. (#107489, #100966, #118371, #117435, #101948, #131645 — @rob-maron, @am423)

Auth & subscriptions

  • Profiles share OAuth logins safely — single-use grants never fork; a profile's own login is kept. (#100929, #134574 — @unsupportedpastels)
  • Nous sign-in survives hourly expiry, concurrent subagents, Portal outages and edge checks. (#103526, #121488 — @kshitijk4poor, @zzragida)
  • Dead logins are named once, with the hermes auth add fix; auth.adopt_external_logins: false stops borrowing Codex CLI/Claude Code logins. (#113816, #115586)
  • Keys stay on their hosts — configured base_url honored everywhere; Anthropic/OpenAI keys never reach foreign hosts. (#121614, #120299 — @austinpickett, @686f6c61, @ahisblessed, @xuezhaolan, @leemove)

Routing/fallback/caching

  • Outage recovery — agent.auto_recovery_cycles (default 5) bounds the wait; fallback returns to a Codex primary whose quota reopened. (#116353, #130716 — @kshitijk4poor, @xxxigm)
  • No surprise billing — auto-routing never bills a provider you didn't pick; provider_routing.models pins OpenRouter per model. (#107366, #104159)
  • Prompt caching — opt-in cache_ttl: auto (1h interactive, 5m subagents/cron); big sessions confirm a cache-breaking /model; compaction keeps the prefix. (#118337, #95267, #104455 — @kshitijk4poor, @JoaoMarcos44)

Reasoning

  • Replay and effort — reasoning replays on every provider that reads it; effort on every picker, custom tier names; Anthropic fast mode follows the docs. (#136358, #110346, #115735, #120517 — @jquesnelle)

🏗️ Core Agent, Sessions & Memory

Agent loop & reliability

  • Stalled turns recover — a watchdog (agent.turn_liveness.timeout_s, default 600 s) interrupts a no-progress turn and frees its session; repeated transcript repairs now raise one error and notice. (#99758, #99687 — @kshitijk4poor, @Finn763, @HexLab98)
  • Streams fail less and report honestly — text-less dead streams retry, repetition loops are cut, and interrupted turns aren't reported as done. (#113135, #130551, #112272 — @kshitijk4poor)

Compression & context

  • Compaction triggers on real token counts — provider-reported usage, not bytes/4; the protected tail is capped at 20% so small-window models compact. (#104555, #117957 — @686f6c61)
  • Bad summaries can't wipe context — refusals and overloaded summary providers leave the transcript alone, and an uncommitted compaction changes nothing. (#118920, #115906, #136083 — @kshitijk4poor, @fangliquanflq, @mnajafian-nv)
  • New knobs — provider-scoped compression.model_thresholds keys and auxiliary.<task>.no_progress_timeout. (#108061, #115804 — @chelsealong)

Sessions & state.db

  • state.db reliability — lock timeouts name the holding process, a busy database no longer kills a turn, and sibling PID namespaces can't steal leases. (#116967, #134577, #132161 — @kshitijk4poor, @686f6c61)
  • state.db stops growing — auto-prune is on by default (ended sessions over 90 days) and closes stale cron/kanban/subagent sessions; VACUUM runs only when 25% is reclaimable. (#101316, #100903 — @stepanov1975)
  • Repair commands — hermes sessions set-journal-mode delete|wal, repair-profiles (crossed profiles) and repair-prompts. (#117684, #115689, #124200 — @kshitijk4poor, @JoaoMarcos44)
  • Cleaner session lists — one-shot runs stay out of pickers, sessions.show_subagents lists titled subagent chats, messages get stable ids for context engines. (#113826, #122304, #126687 — @OutThisLife, @kshitijk4poor, @100yenadmin)

Memory

  • Memory providers leave core safely — OpenViking and Holographic move to the plugin catalog on update or first start, data intact, or say why not. (#134350, #136436, #131269)
  • Built-in memory writes land — hand-formatted MEMORY.md/USER.md accepted, refusals say what to fix, secrets scrubbed before providers see them. (#133038, #134429 — @aniruddhaadak80)

Profiles & config

  • config.yaml survives bad moments — a broken file falls back to the last good copy; read errors can't wipe it. (#109463, #120343 — @Shenrui-Ma, @huklaa)
  • Safer hermes config — get masks secrets (--raw opts out); set refuses typo paths and stores env-style names in .env. (#111341, #112250 — @webtecnica)
  • Profiles stay separate — REST routes act on the profile they name, exports skip .ssh/.aws/.gnupg, and import-agent --sync keeps imports current. (#118275, #132201, #88855 — @kshitijk4poor, @JerryLiu369)

Languages (i18n)

  • Language packs — any language can ship as a plugin (provides_locales) or a per-profile override file. (#126296)

📱 Messaging Platforms (Gateway)

Gateway core & multiplexing

  • One gateway serves every profile — hermes gateway status explains any boot-guard refusal, hermes gateway migrate --multiplex migrates by hand, webhook platforms serve each profile at /p/<profile>/, hermes -p X gateway stop|start parks one profile, and gateway.standalone: true is a temporary opt-out. (#112854, #108952, #119774, #119680 — @victor-kyriazakos)
  • Profiles stay isolated — clones skip bot tokens unless --clone-channels; profile_routes can route by sender. (#109502, #115642 — @italodsfernandes)
  • Sessions and commands — /login signs free-tier users in from a DM; idle/daily session_reset is retired, so only /new and /reset start fresh; /branch opens a sibling thread on Discord, Telegram, Slack and Matrix (--here keeps the old behavior); decline mode sends unauthorized DMs one polite refusal; an opt-in served_model footer names the model that answered. (#105261, #126244, #117450, #88028, #115925 — @alt-glitch, @kshitijk4poor, @anpicasso)
  • Files and plugins — MEDIA: files from ssh/Modal/Daytona sandboxes reach the user; plugins can claim messages via post_gateway_admission. (#103600, #133157 — @kshitijk4poor, @tokou)

Messaging platforms

  • Telegram — opt-in bots_require_mention stops bot reply loops, extra.drop_pending_on_cold_boot: false keeps messages sent while offline, CJK can opt in to rich messages. (#106534, #116134, #111831 — @kshitijk4poor, @kokhlo, @justcarlosm, @wang2)
  • Discord — setup verifies the token, links the Message Content Intent and allowlists you; free-response channels can auto-thread. (#132456, #116168 — @kshitijk4poor, @FunJim)
  • Slack — Block Kit /model picker; status/titles use the Agent Sessions API. (#103436, #101477 — @kshitijk4poor, @sohomsahaun)
  • WhatsApp — native mentions via hermes send --mention, no 5 s batching delay, paired sessions on every profile. (#115253, #117104, #132518 — @kshitijk4poor, @unsupportedpastels, @Diaspar4u)
  • Signal, Matrix, Email, QQ Bot — Signal tables render monospace; Matrix LaTeX typesets in Element (Labs math setting); Email refuses spoofed senders and now requires an authserv_id pin (mail drops until set); QQ Bot send_message delivers media. (#106538, #106515, #132663, #131927 — @chelsealong, @romanovzky, @kshitijk4poor, @OutThisLife, @cadezhou)

Webhooks & API server

  • Webhooks — opt-in coalesce folds event bursts into one run, cron_job routes fire cron jobs on events, Standard Webhooks signatures verify, opt-in mirror_to_session gives replies context. (#92066, #95262, #104183, #120825 — @HwangJohn, @Potrock)
  • API server — reasoning streams to OpenAI-compatible clients; stream approvals are answerable. (#115797, #121769 — @kshitijk4poor, @liuhao1024)

Relay & Bot Chat

  • Bot Mode rooms — edit members, Reply to @handle, 200-message history, sender-aware bot DMs with an ALLOW_BOTS loop guard. (#113384, #107520 — @erosika, @69k4xmdfm2-blip)
  • Relay — connector checks send_message targets; NeMo Relay tracing at 0.10.0 tags spans with the working directory. (#99220, #135787, #108345 — @benbarclay, @mnajafian-nv, @afourniernv, @KoNit-K)

🖥️ Desktop App

Workspace & layout

  • Simple / Advanced mode — Simple hides developer panes; Advanced stays the default. (#118587 — @OutThisLife)
  • Windows and tabs — Titlebar tabs; profiles open in their own window or as startup default. (#107099, #113176 — @OutThisLife)
  • Sidebar — Group by gateway/profile, reuse Cloud instances, drag New session onto a pane. (#105813, #70571 — @abundantbeing)
  • Import sessions — Continue another coding agent's transcript in Hermes. (#104229 — @Adolanium)
  • Goal / Loop / Heartbeat cards — Live pause/resume/stop above the composer. (#104246 — @jerrygooch)
  • Launch options — Start on a fresh chat; opt-in tray minimize and Cmd+Option / Ctrl+Alt HUD tap. (#101304, #118441, #118446 — @jinglun010-cpu, @OutThisLife)

Chat & composer

  • Pickers and input — Reasoning-effort pill; add a model by slug; 3,000+ char pastes attach as .txt; opt-in macOS window-screenshot shortcut. (#109907, #120693, #108901, #113349 — @OutThisLife)
  • Actionable errors — Cards name the problem and fix; usage-limit cards show reset time and can schedule a retry. (#111601, #116288)
  • Transcript — Stable approval queue; hideable code diffs. (#107073, #116569 — @OutThisLife)
  • Browser comment mode — Pin page elements with notes; crops, selectors and styles go to the composer. (#100083 — @Adolanium)

Settings & plugins

  • Settings — Ordered subpages searchable from ⌘K; Chat Font (e.g. OpenDyslexic); Russian, French, German, Spanish. (#117127, #111226, #100429 — @OutThisLife, @warment, @FASTCHIP)
  • Models — Per-task auxiliary reasoning effort; Custom Endpoints pick an API mode. (#110435, #115851 — @higgs1729, @SacrEllfarch)
  • Plugins — One row per plugin; composer and settings APIs. (#107314, #120907 — @OutThisLife, @Heybinshao)

Bot Mode

  • Bot Screen — Linux-gateway bots stream a desktop you can take over for logins or 2FA. Off by default; needs host packages. (#108914)
  • Group chats — Reorder rooms; compress a member's hidden history. (#105909, #117015)

Performance & platforms

  • One backend — Desktop attaches to the running backend, which serves every profile. (#118246)
  • Platforms — Faster Windows startup; Linux runs without sudo and under WSLg/Wayland. (#111002, #100136, #113247 — @codeJRV, @kvnloo, @austinpickett)

Models, chat & workspace

  • Model picker knows your plans — Star Favorites; provider rows show when a rate limit lifts and a "17% left · resets 3:00 PM" chip before you hit it. Also opt-in per-model pricing with cached-read rates, an Ultrafast speed tier and keys to step reasoning effort. (#126535, #133392, #133408, #126651, #132445, #125800 — @OutThisLife, @kshitijk4poor, @anotherchu)
  • Safer rewinds and honest stops — Regenerate or edit asks before archiving more than one later turn; Stop shows the partial reply the session saves. (#133748, #121597 — @kshitijk4poor)
  • Reading and layout — Chat Text Size separate from UI Scale; theme typography; lightbox zoom/pan; compression count in the context meter; context-aware right-click. (#128975, #125904, #127247, #126653, #131215 — @OutThisLife, @unsupportedpastels, @dskwe)
  • Workspace and sessions — Sessions follow the agent's git worktree and branch; Review pane scopes work. Browse SSH workspaces, drag sessions onto projects, search logs, see and clear Kanban blocks. (#128428, #127200, #99538, #126543, #126658, #125647 — @ethernet8023, @OutThisLife, @nateEc)
  • Preview and voice — Preview opens public web pages; YouTube plays via a local player. Non-streaming TTS speaks from the first sentence; quiet-audio STT hallucinations are filtered. (#130393, #130975, #121685, #127275 — @xxxigm, @OutThisLife, @kshitijk4poor, @embwl0x)

Plugins, updates & the app

  • Settings ▸ Plugins and a bigger SDK — Every plugin gets a settings page (agent plugins from their config schema). Desktop plugins can run app actions by id, speak via the pet, add Appearance controls and reorder sidebar nav. (#133182, #136417, #135504, #120912, #120922 — @tobenwarrior)
  • Updates you can steer — Source installs pick Stable or Every commit in Settings › About; the updates dialog names the channel. The app warns when older than its backend. (#135949, #136313, #122911 — @OutThisLife)
  • App polish — "While working" keep-awake mode, macOS 26 layered icon, sharper MSIX icons, opt-in shared-metrics consent strip. (#128984, #122976, #131669, #125074 — @kshitijk4poor, @jonpol01, @ethernet8023)

⌨️ CLI, TUI & Dashboard

CLI

  • Vim keys, git branch and bell — display.vim_mode, git_branch status field, display.bell_on_prompt (bell plus OSC 9 notification); all off by default. (#106412, #106434, #101284, #101320 — @saforem2, @TurgutKural, @glitchbunny0, @harshmoney123)
  • Friendlier keys — Ctrl+T opens the subagent monitor; a mid-turn multi-line paste is one message. (#105879, #109146)
  • Scriptable commands — hermes usage --json (quota, no session); hermes worktree --json/--older-than; kanban edit --title/--body/--priority; -t picks MCP servers to start. (#115899, #95257, #117632, #101634 — @himanusia, @kshitijk4poor, @SelfParody)
  • Also — hermes -z exits non-zero on failure; -m custom:<name>:<model> reaches that provider; the footer no longer collapses to "Window too small". (#113234, #115293, #117439 — @kshitijk4poor)

TUI

  • Native terminal mode — --native or display.tui_native (opt-in). (#121035 — @OutThisLife)
  • Running-turn control — double-Esc interrupts, push-to-talk cuts speech, the live dock (CLI too) shows /goal and queued prompts. (#125803 — @OutThisLife)
  • Typing fixes — Shift+letter on extended-key terminals, macOS Ctrl+D, Wayland image paste, steady idle redraws. (#90674, #116851, #116824, #87907 — @francip, @fangliquanflq)
  • Reconnects and attachments — attached TUIs rejoin their session; attachments.storage: workspace stages files a sandboxed agent can read. (#112227, #125816 — @OutThisLife)

Dashboard

  • Chat on more devices — screen readers, iPad touch-scroll, no doubled mobile-keyboard characters; a second tab gets its own terminal. (#133960, #134181, #116978, #117061 — @austinpickett)
  • Errors, sessions, analytics — errors are a sentence and fix button, not raw JSON; title search, a live switcher, per-API-call analytics. (#111602, #133981, #134100, #133991 — @austinpickett)
  • Self-hosting — reverse-proxy subpaths, password sign-in beside OAuth, non-expiring remote sessions. (#133903, #107018, #110061 — @austinpickett, @Doud-FR)

Slash commands

  • Reasoning effort in every picker — hermes model, aux models and /model add an effort step; /model X --reasoning high works too. (#110346)
  • Editable /queue — list, edit, move or remove prompts; /s = /steer. (#95999, #125803 — @abhinav11082001-stack, @OutThisLife)
  • /context per file — shows each instruction file as loaded, truncated or shadowed. (#91272)

🔧 Tools, MCP & Browser

Tools & tool search

  • Rare tools load on demand — computer_use, session_search, image_generate, cron and Desktop GUI tools sit behind tool_search by default (desktop schemas 13,367→6,883 tokens; defer: [] opts out); todo/cronjob/process become todo_list/cronjob_manage/process_manage (aliases kept). (#97979 — @alt-glitch)
  • Prompt cache holds all session — the tool list is frozen per session across TUI, -q --resume and gateway hops. (#120289)
  • Loop guardrails — unattended gateway/cron sessions hard-stop tool loops; 5 identical replays halt any tool. (#100967 — @plcunha, @benbenlijie)
  • Smarter file and web tools — read_file shows SQLite schemas and flags merge conflicts, search_files runs ripgrep directly (5–6x faster), keyless web_search fails over between free vendors. (#115974, #104502, #134048 — @kshitijk4poor, @alt-glitch)

MCP

  • Device-code login — hermes mcp login <server> --flow device for headless terminals. (#104891 — @wjorgensen)
  • Sturdier OAuth — refresh tokens are never erased, double-spent or sent to another issuer; RFC 9207, Google-hosted and WAF-fronted servers log in; gateways never open browser tabs. (#106185, #111186 — @kshitijk4poor, @anhtahaylove)
  • More servers connect — SSE-only servers fall back, strict servers connect (sampling.tools opt-in), and the catalog adds official n8n and Asana V2. (#106432, #131891, #116048 — @aieng-abdullah, @OutThisLife, @nikkoxgonzales, @alt-glitch)
  • Startup and results — boot connects ≤ mcp.discovery_concurrency (4) servers at once; vision models see MCP images; dead stdio servers respawn without replaying a call. (#117956, #133815, #106546 — @Halldrix, @sarahwu185, @100yenadmin)

Browser & computer use

  • Vault and sandboxed screen — password-manager sign-in also fills cards and addresses after you confirm; hermes-sandbox:desktop is the new default image (existing sandboxes kept). (#106480, #121169)
  • browser_exec works out of the box — drives bundled Chromium (also the cloud fallback) and opens localhost via a sidecar. (#102986 — @shadus)
  • Also: a CDP seam for plugins; unchanged computer_use screens are not resent. (#133552, #98954 — @web3blind)

Terminal & code execution

  • Long commands stop blocking — foreground timeouts over 600 s become background jobs, heartbeat=N sends progress, and the live-work dock lists them. (#103529)
  • Sandboxes — results flag a recreated environment; SSH paths resolve remotely. (#107093, #122657 — @kshitijk4poor, @xxxigm)
  • LSP — custom servers via lsp.servers and lsp.package_manager; never runs a cloned repo's own interpreter or SDK. (#115714, #126239 — @ZHCHJ888, @kshitijk4poor, @jonpol01)

Media (image/video/voice)

  • New models — GPT Image 2.5, Kling Image v3; video: Kling 3.0, Kling O3, LTX 2.5, Wan 3.0, MiniMax H3 Max Turbo, Gemini Omni Flash 1.1. (#105988)
  • Live dictation — opt-in stt.streaming shows words as you speak (CLI, TUI, Desktop); long recordings transcribe on all cloud providers. (#133853, #133825 — @TimeLord2010, @Julientalbot, @kokhlo)
  • Voice conversations — opt-in voice.voice_chat_mode: gpt-live gives Desktop full-duplex voice; auxiliary.voice_chat runs spoken turns on a faster model; TTS starts sooner. (#108137, #133986, #115846 — @liuhao1024)

🧩 Plugins, Skills & the Plugin Catalog

Plugin Catalog

  • Plugin and author pages — /docs/plugins shelves plugins under Memory, Desktop, Platforms, Web, Tools, Voice, Automation and Models. Pages show the description, a disclosure callout, screenshots, the README from the pinned commit, an Open in Hermes Desktop button and a copyable install command. (#111415, #111547, #117808)
  • Easier-to-read cards — a version label sits next to the pinned commit (1.2.0 @ ad3f352b) on the website, CLI and Desktop; cards gained banner images, a uniform size and light mode. (#113381, #125065, #124001 — @kshitijk4poor, @xxxigm)
  • Known-issue warnings — listings can declare known issues, which hermes plugins install and the dashboard show on install. (#124058 — @happy5318)
  • Listing rules — Desktop plugins must stay within SDK slots, 11 plugins that patched internals were delisted (installed copies keep working), and the submission guide matches what CI enforces. (#115579, #130860, #131048)

New in the Catalog

All 600 listings are new this cycle.

  • Moved out of core — besides the five memory providers, Holographic has a new community maintainer; Home Assistant, Spotify, Langfuse and TouchDesigner are now official catalog plugins. (#114732, #136196, #132476, #137130 — @itpartypattaya)
  • Official — Blender Lab, Snyk, NVIDIA App and NVIDIA Broadcast (Windows only), Telegram Business, Memory Wiki. (#119365, #109084 — @alt-glitch)
  • Memory (65) — Cognee, LanceDB, ReMe, Memoria, HydraDB, Mnemosyne, YantrikDB, BetterDB. Web and search (34) — Apify, Parallel, Linkup, SerpApi, You.com, TinyFish, Oxylabs. Model providers (48) — JetBrains Junie, Telnyx, NetMind, Groq, Chutes, Kiro, Opper, AIHubMix. Platforms (32) — Inline, Microsoft 365, Rocket.Chat, VK, Zalo, iMessage, Sendblue. Voice (18) — Speechify, Deepgram, Fish Audio, Gemini Live. Desktop panes (140) — Telegram Client, Quota, Orbit, Kanban Gantt, Monitoring Dashboard. Tools (180) — GitHub App, Coder, Devin, NetBox, Financial Datasets, MetaMask, Tempo MPP, DaVinci Resolve, vidIQ, Govee, SwitchBot, Japanese e-Gov data. (#112292, #118231, #118512, #114202 — @siillee, @georgeatparallel, @AlexanderPrendota and many more)

Plugin system

  • Model providers as plugins — out-of-tree providers show in model pickers, log in via hermes auth (built-in OAuth PKCE) and refresh tokens. (#116552 — @ericmaddox, @ZenMux-1)
  • Per-profile plugin host (opt-in) — plugins.isolation: host runs third-party plugins in a per-profile process with only that profile's secrets. (#126911)
  • New plugin APIs — hooks for interrupted turns, auxiliary LLM calls and human-input prompts; plugins can start gateway sessions, add Automation Blueprints and aux model slots, ship the computer-use driver and make Codex-signed requests. (#118909 — @zrmnelson, @JonZal, @hsy5571615, @webtecnica)
  • Desktop plugin SDK — config_schema settings render as a form in Capabilities → Plugins; plugins get a skills/toolsets/profiles bridge and model-pill/menu slots. (#118917 — @LeonSGP43, @tobenwarrior)
  • Install, pin, update — private repos install with your git credentials, Desktop pins a plugin to a commit, updates keep user data, and plugin skills run as /plugin:skill. (#106981, #107321, #133175 — @JoaoMarcos44, @pnpwin, @ctaylor86)
  • Sturdier loader and scanner — hung imports are skipped after plugins.load_timeout_seconds (10s), guard hooks fail closed, and prose, fixtures and regex literals no longer block installs; plugin deps follow the plugin's security policy. (#118915, #115954)
  • Moved out of core — Spotify and Langfuse became official plugins (auto-migrated); tirith was removed, its key checks built in. (#135389, #133832)
  • New plugins — reMarkable, hermes-pii, hermes-stt-enhance (catalog); Meta muse-image (bundled). (#117191, #132154, #134418, #100959 — @cygnostik, @lukefx, @dmmeteo, @mreso)

Skills

  • New optional skills — pr-lens (animated PR diagrams), system-atlas, anatomy (isometric explainers), mono-color (ink-print images), dream-loop (3D concept-art loops), scrollcraft (scroll pages), auteur (cinematic web), ip-as-logo (mascots), ai-presenter-video, live-dashboard (self-updating pages), dynamic-workflow (fan-out campaigns), reddit-reading, rss-feeds. (#109306, #103821)
  • Skills Hub — the index builds again with about 480 science skills; search also finds unindexed skills. (#109178)
  • skills.auto_load and skills.create_dir — pin skills into every session; choose where agent-made skills go. (#92048, #100377 — @ctaylor86, @ArcherQAQ, @giwaov, @Frosti7)
  • Better self-improvement — learnings and corrections go into the task's skill; background review can improve any skill; skills added mid-chat reach the next message. (#102107)
  • Lighter curator — no longer blocks the CLI prompt; one install fell from 1.9 GB to about 200 MB; unused skills archive at 30 days (was 90). (#115862)
  • Skills Guard — blocked installs in a 421-skill sample fell from 83 to 68; inline shell is limited to non-community skills. (#135595 — @Awshesh12, @liuhao1024, @FlashWayne)

⏰ Cron, Kanban & Delegation

Cron

  • Jobs follow your main model — Unpinned jobs use the model configured at fire time; pinned locks one. (#113679)
  • Self-healing schedules — A fire that never reached the model (e.g. after wake) re-runs after 5, 15 and 30 minutes, a provider "retry after" window parks the job quietly, and a slot missed during a restart fires once. (#108731, #115843)
  • Quieter alerts — One alert per incident plus a reminder every cron.failure_repeat_alert_hours; failure_deliver reroutes or mutes failure notices; an agent can fail its run with [CRON_FAILURE]. (#114807, #101373, #113155) — @victor-kyriazakos
  • New job options — --paused creates a job unarmed; --interpreter runs scripts on your own venv. (#104984, #125392) — @M1racleShih
  • Profiles and delivery — A multiplexed gateway runs each profile's jobs with that profile's env and secrets; delivery needs positive evidence, output is secret-redacted everywhere, and late or satellite runs are labelled. (#118027, #100908, #112830) — @yoniebans

Kanban

  • Workers ride out provider trouble — Failed turns retry in place with context; rate limits or 5xx requeue instead of burning retries. (#112318, #112500) — @EvgenyMettsler, @dmelkk-secondbrain
  • A board that explains itself — Cards show the worker's last output on failure; dispatch says why a card is held. (#112618) — @chelsealong
  • Workflow and tooling — One workflow definition drives columns; workers get kanban_schedule; kanban create --body-file; Desktop follows gateway switches. (#134340, #130455, #116791, #119054) — @benbarclay, @unsupportedpastels, @fangliquanflq

Delegation & multi-agent

  • Live subagents, images and /stop (see Highlights). (#105593)
  • Fan-outs lose less — Failed children surface at once, an owner crash keeps finished work, children can hand processes to the parent, and per-task completions are opt-in. (#103549, #116707, #105125)
  • Child routing — delegation.fallback_providers gives children their own chain; child_timeout_seconds measures inactivity. (#105347, #117606) — @ayushnangia
  • /goal — Consistent on CLI, Desktop and messaging; the judge waits on subagents; /goal pause for now no longer overwrites the goal; gates run in the session workspace. (#104833, #132251, #125453) — @jayzhou2309

🔒 Security, Privacy & Codebase

Security & privacy

  • v0.21.6 security fixes — dashboard sign-in hardening (spoofed X-Forwarded-For, size caps, loopback-only login codes); automatic git skips repo filter programs; the email allowlist resists display-name spoofing. (#133367, #130685, #130661, #125212) — @unsupportedpastels, @kshitijk4poor, @Froraut, @jonpol01, @keeltrace
  • Secrets stay put — child processes don't inherit adapter/dashboard secrets; remote execute_code keeps its token out of argv; profile exports skip credential stores; keys never reach foreign hosts on refresh/fallback. (#125755, #124262, #130525, #120299) — @kshitijk4poor, @jonpol01, @beardthelion, @leemove
  • Sharper redaction — debug share redacts before truncating; file reads and terminal output mask .env/config secrets; hermes config get masks unless --raw; memory hand-offs are scrubbed; plain token= lines stay visible. (#132270, #111422, #111341, #134429, #100564) — @kshitijk4poor, @Froraut, @shehjaddev, @webtecnica, @aniruddhaadak80, @fangliquanflq
  • Owner-only files at rest — credentials, state.db, snapshots, browser profiles, media caches, any umask. (#109509, #127229) — @JoaoMarcos44, @rohitsabu, @OutThisLife
  • Wider guards — more fetches pass the SSRF guard (security.fake_ip_ranges for fake-ip proxies); cloud-metadata fetches, zsh/dash pipe-to-shell and deno eval need approval; approvals.deny works in sandboxes; built-in rules replace bundled tirith. (#114887, #113156, #116726, #103649, #133832) — @beardthelion, @AYin-Z, @fangliquanflq
  • Holes closed — the MCP OAuth callback stops reflecting HTML; webhook, SMS and WhatsApp Cloud inbound reject blank signing secrets. (#92612, #133257) — @memosr, @ahadvice
  • Opt-in shared metrics — daily usage stats reach Nous only with consent; both telemetry.shared_metrics switches default off, each profile asks once, Enter = "No thanks". (#95278) — @benbarclay

Codebase & performance

  • Leaner prompts and tools — ~2.1k fewer prompt tokens a request, on-demand vault tools, one-round-trip file probes, hermes -w no longer repacks each launch. (#137084, #101599) — @yaojiejia

Other

  • Safer backups — hermes backup keeps the newest 3, flags incomplete archives; hermes import refuses damaged ones. (#106455, #121498) — @buihongduc132, @kshitijk4poor
  • Also — KEY_2… env vars join credential pools; one-screen hermes status; opt-in display.suppress_warning_notifications; Desktop Bots roster sections; only Hermes' own node/npm/uv are used; Xiaomi MiMo v2.6. (#112390, #131034, #114370, #101290, #125589, #118502) — @PhoeniXAbhisheK, @victor-kyriazakos, @fortun8te, @rob-maron
  • For contributors — CODEOWNERS replaces the label gate; E2E (incl. native Windows) runs only on release builds; stable releases skip macOS Intel/Termux by default. (#131726, #136603) — @ethernet8023

🐛 Notable Bug Fixes

Onboarding, connectors & local models

  • Managed llama-server no longer crashes on Windows with 0xC0000374 and no longer inherits your credentials (#116923)
  • Windows local-model servers are contained and orphans recovered safely; two backends booting together start one router (#111038, #117600 — @jquesnelle, @chelsealong)

Install, update & packaging

  • Docker agents no longer lose their built-in API server, messaging and MCP dependencies after a restart. (#135726 — @unsupportedpastels)
  • A hermes update killed mid-pull no longer breaks every entry point. (#120339)

Providers & models

  • A rate-limited Claude model no longer benches the whole credential for every other Claude model. (#113171 — @KoNit-K)
  • Codex app-server sessions now get SOUL.md and the Hermes system prompt. (#115759 — @JonthanaHanh, @ooiuuii, @Momentum96)

Agent loop, sessions & memory

  • A chat whose session row was deleted recreates it instead of silently dropping every later turn (#124117 — @kshitijk4poor, @strzhao)
  • Messages flushed at gateway shutdown are recovered into their session (#114225 — @kshitijk4poor, @spfcraze, @yoyodine-industries, @briandevans)

Gateway & platforms

  • Telegram no longer stalls under flood control or stays connected but deaf on a wedged dispatcher. (#116987, #132162 — @kshitijk4poor)
  • Slack native streaming stops posting answers twice. (#121762 — @kshitijk4poor, @ms-elbdev)

Desktop

  • Replies no longer show twice, garble or vanish after resume, steer, a chat switch or reconnect (#118890, #120290, #121061 — @BearHuddleston)
  • Pressing Enter answers the clarify card on screen, not a background chat's (#76472 — @briandevans)
  • Duplicate or vanishing replies after refresh, session switch or reused tool-call ids (#131758, #128003, #130553 — @OutThisLife, @alt-glitch, @kshitijk4poor)
  • "This device" backends no longer idle-killed after an hour; new chats run on the gateway you picked (#134942, #132538 — @unsupportedpastels)

CLI, TUI & dashboard

  • Dashboard saves no longer overwrite secrets with their masked preview (#121250 — @kshitijk4poor)
  • Resizing the CLI no longer re-appends the conversation to scrollback (#120321 — @Jackal991)

Tools, MCP & browser

  • A parked OAuth MCP server no longer replays its dead refresh token on every new session, a storm that could trip a rate-limit ban (#137502).
  • computer_use element clicks work again on cua-driver 0.21+; only pixel clicks worked before (#111313 — @jeffjhunter).

Plugins & skills

  • hermes plugins list and the dashboard Plugins page make at most one live catalog request instead of waiting through one timeout per installed plugin. (#114811)
  • On treeless source checkouts the plugin inventory no longer takes 40–80 seconds, which was longer than the Desktop request timeout. (#125716 — @liuhao1024)
  • Enabling a plugin from Desktop, the TUI or the dashboard no longer strips the core tools. (#135048)
  • A platform whose plugin failed to load at startup is flagged in hermes status and comes back without a restart. (#130613, #133289) — @kshitijk4poor

Cron, Kanban & delegation

  • Gateway /goal gate add no longer lets a non-admin reach the host shell (#101499)
  • Automatic git in Kanban, worktree cleanup and subagents no longer runs a cloned repo's hooks or filters (#130661)

Security & misc

  • Qwen models behind Alibaba's content guardrail end the turn with the safety notice instead of retrying for four minutes. (#136425)
  • Detected password managers can be switched on from Settings again; the toggle was a no-op. (#109558) — @kokhlo

↩️ Reverted in this window (not shipping)

  • Collective Wisdom V1 (an in-tree org skill marketplace) was merged and then removed; there is no wisdom code in this release. (#94266 — @shannonsands)
  • The Desktop card-style Skills/Plugins catalog browser and its website "Install in Hermes" links were reverted three times; Desktop browses capabilities as list tabs with the embedded catalog picker. (#119381, #131252)
  • Approval and clarify prompts that wait until answered were reverted; approvals.timeout still applies. (#130167)
  • Parallel group-chat rounds in Bot Mode were reverted; rooms run serially. (#101112, #101139)
  • Full isolation of named profiles from the root auth.json was reverted after Desktop bots lost their shared login; profiles keep their own login when they have one. (#113425, #118373, #134574)
  • hermes cron resnap and creation-time model snapshots were replaced by cron following your main model (pinned locks a job to one model). (#106496, #113679)
  • The Classic default skin palette and a tirith catalog plugin were reverted. (#130015, #133838)

v2.11.29v2.11.29 — CLI and tooling fixes from @rudycelekli, each checked against the vendor's current docs. Stable2026-10-14T01:41:43Z

CLI and tooling fixes from @rudycelekli, each checked against the vendor's current docs.

  • Request deadlines (#751): HTTP calls in 65 CLIs now time out instead of hanging. The default is 120 seconds, raised from #751's 30 so long scrapes and exports aren't cut off; override it with --timeout-ms.
  • Mixpanel regions (#744): US, EU, and India ingestion, query, and export hosts.
  • Payload validation before sending: the CLIs now reject inputs the vendor's API would refuse anyway. Valid empty and nested values still pass.
    • Amplitude event properties (#801)
    • SendGrid dynamic template data (#803)
    • Kit custom fields (#806)
    • Segment properties and traits (#808)
    • Customer.io data objects (#811)
    • Mixpanel properties (#813)
    • Resend batch size (#816) and template variables (#828)
    • OneSignal conflicting audiences (#818)
    • Intercom event metadata (#822)
    • Dub bulk link limits (#824)
    • Pendo aggregation envelopes (#830)
  • Correctness: Close opportunity amounts keep exact integers instead of truncating (#831).
    • Interface change: amplitude --properties '' now errors instead of being ignored (#801).
  • Tooling:
    • Packaging checks file sizes before loading them (#804).
    • The README sync handles Windows line endings (#805).
    • check-versions catches orphaned nested references and broken sibling links (#807).

Skill ZIP assets

Complete source catalog: 50 skills. Claude web target: 50 skills with reviewed short descriptions.

Choose <name>-claude-web.zip for the documented single-folder web upload format. Source ZIPs retain original metadata; they are not substitutes for omitted web ZIPs. Live client upload, account limits, and invocation behavior have not been verified.

Web omissions (also recorded in claude-web-manifest.json):

  • None.

v2.11.28v2.11.28 — Skill fixes from @rudycelekli. A reviewer recomputed every calculation, and the guidance… Stable2026-10-14T00:45:08Z

Skill fixes from @rudycelekli. A reviewer recomputed every calculation, and the guidance stays firm where a change would otherwise have hedged it.

  • Math:
    • offers (1.0.1 → 1.0.2): multiply sequential lifts rather than adding them (#809).
    • churn-prevention (2.0.2 → 2.0.3): dunning ROI stated net of tooling, at 5–14x (#810). Health scores put every signal on one 0–100 scale and handle missing signals explicitly (#829).
    • co-marketing (2.0.2 → 2.0.3): marketplace fees are recurring costs, not CAC, and Arrows is listed on the HubSpot Marketplace (#812).
    • referrals (2.2.0 → 2.2.1): program return measured after serving costs (#820).
    • ab-testing (2.0.1 → 2.0.2): test duration accounts for the number of arms and exposure (#819).
  • Methods:
    • attribution (1.1.4 → 1.1.5): intent-to-treat analysis vs. on-off tests, and an interval that includes zero is not a win (#815).
    • revops (2.2.0 → 2.2.1): calibrate scoring on snapshots taken at decision time and on holdouts (#817).
    • pricing (2.1.3 → 2.1.4): the direction of the Van Westendorp "too cheap" curves (#821).
    • onboarding (2.0.1 → 2.0.2): early activation measured separately from mature retention (#826). Card-required trials judged on visitor→paid, with ChartMogul's ~30% trial→paid (over 5x no-card) as context (#827).
  • Platform facts:
    • seo-audit (2.2.0 → 2.2.1): hreflang findings scoped to the affected pairs, and x-default treated as optional (#823).
    • ads (2.4.6 → 2.4.7): screen headlines in Meta, then prove the winner with a landing-page test, without the made-up lift thresholds (#825).

Skill ZIP assets

Complete source catalog: 50 skills. Claude web target: 50 skills with reviewed short descriptions.

<p align="center"> <sub><b>Edge users:</b> Voyager is still maintained on Edge Add-ons for users who need Edge on mobile or tablet. If review is delayed, Chrome Web Store and GitHub manual packages remain available.</sub> </p>

  • Chrome / Edge / Chromium browsers: voyager-chrome-v2.0.0.zip
  • Firefox: voyager-firefox-v2.0.0.xpi
  • Safari: voyager-v2.0.0.dmg

Do not use GitHub's auto-generated Source code (zip/tar.gz) archives for manual extension installs. Those files are repository source snapshots, not browser-ready extension packages.

<details> <summary><b>🍎 Safari 安装与升级说明 (Safari installation & upgrade)</b></summary>

🎉 从 v1.6.0 起，Voyager 的核心功能已正式覆盖 Safari。 Starting with v1.6.0, Voyager's core experience is officially supported on Safari.

  • 📦 安装 (Installation): 下载并打开 voyager-v2.0.0.dmg，按提示安装应用。 Download and open voyager-v2.0.0.dmg, then follow the prompts to install the app.
  • ⚠️ 旧版升级 (Legacy upgrade): 如果仍安装着 Gemini Voyager.app，请先完成一次 Safari 迁移步骤，避免两个 App 并存。 If Gemini Voyager.app is still installed, follow the one-time Safari migration guide first so the two apps do not conflict.
  • 🖼️ 图片能力 (Images): Image Refinement 水印去除、全尺寸图片下载与聊天导出中的图片提取均已支持。 Image Refinement watermark removal, full-size image downloads, and image extraction in conversation exports are supported.
  • ☁️ 原生云同步 (Native cloud sync): Google Drive 与 iCloud 同步均已接入 Safari 原生层。 Google Drive and iCloud sync are integrated through Safari's native layer.
  • 🔔 系统通知 (Notifications): Gemini 回复完成后可发送 macOS 通知，并直达对应对话。 macOS can notify you when a Gemini response finishes and open the matching conversation.
  • 🔄 自动更新 (Automatic updates): 完成这次迁移后，后续版本可通过 Sparkle 自动更新。 After this one-time migration, future versions can update automatically through Sparkle.
