Graphify-Labs/graphify ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.9.90 Stable2026-10-13T22:17:06Z

0.9.90 (2026-10-13)

Fourteen community pull requests: C# call-resolution and id-collision fixes, Python/Go/dedup correctness, a hub-label sidecar, a post-merge heal hook, and query/build fixes. The AST cache schema is bumped, so the first build after upgrading re-extracts.

  • Fix: C# a nameless member-call receiver (run.Sequence.Apply(), Make().Apply()) no longer mis-binds to the caller's own same-named method; every C# member call defers and resolves only through a typed receiver (#4422, thanks @nixvideojuego-ui).
  • Fix: C# new X() no longer binds to a method that shares the type's name, same-file or cross-file; a real same-named type still wins (#4428, thanks @nixvideojuego-ui).
  • Feature: C# foreach loop variables are now typed from the collection's element type (arrays, known sequences, dictionary .Keys/.Values), so calls on the loop variable resolve; untypable iterables stay unresolved (#4423, thanks @nixvideojuego-ui).
  • Fix: C# node ids now keep names that differ only by a leading/trailing _ or by case apart (Action/_Action, Load/load, Token/TOKEN), in both ids and member-call resolution keys, instead of collapsing one onto the other (#4415, #4416, thanks @JensD-git).
  • Fix: C# colliding ids are salted before the partial-class merge, so a partial half keeps its members and an unrelated node sharing a half's id is no longer deleted (#4411, #4412, thanks @JensD-git).
  • Fix: Go a generic receiver type (func (b *Box[T]) ...) is normalized before building its id, so a generic type's methods attach to the type node instead of a separate Box[T] node (#4438, thanks @Goodkamalpandey).
  • Fix: Python a module member call (mod.func()) now resolves when the caller is a method or nested function, by walking the enclosing scopes to the file node instead of reading only the immediate parent (#2447, #4437, thanks @SrijanSriv).
  • Fix: an edge between two surviving nodes is no longer dropped just because its own source_file was not dispatched this run; it is kept as long as both endpoints are kept nodes (#2276, #4436, thanks @SrijanSriv).
  • Fix: dedup keeps a dropped-folder document next to a top-level file that mints the same id (protocol.md beside docs/protocol.md and docs/firmware/protocol.md), path-qualifying the siblings instead of collapsing and losing one; a genuine reference still collapses onto the named file (#4281, #4410, thanks @Deepjyoti-Sarmah).
  • Fix: Jinja templates (.j2, .jinja, .jinja2) are now classified as documents instead of being dropped from the graph (#3427, #3455, thanks @nikhilsaxena04).
  • Fix: build source-less placeholder nodes orphaned by a re-extraction that drops their edges are now swept on a normal (non-prune) incremental build, not just under prune; the sweep removes only code placeholders and external stubs and spares disk-isolated, fresh-isolated, and hyperedge-member nodes (#4399, thanks @Mpasha17).
  • Fix: query the fallback traversal now retains relationships among the visited nodes (seed-to-seed edges, cycles, cross-links, and parallel relations), via the induced subgraph, instead of printing only tree edges (#4314, #4315, thanks @vibhanshu-mishra).
  • Feature: watch hub-fallback community labels now go to a .graphify_labels.pending.json sidecar, so the tracked .graphify_labels.json holds only curated (manual/LLM) names; a promoted name drops from the sidecar and an emptied sidecar is removed (#3334, #3551, thanks @ayushcodes10).
  • Feature: hooks graphify hook install now also installs a post-merge hook that runs an unscoped background rebuild after a merge, healing a symbol the graph.json union merge driver resurrected on one side (which nothing else would re-extract), so the phantom node no longer persists (#2418, #3556, thanks @ayushcodes10).

thedotmack/claude-mem ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v13.39.0 Stable2026-10-13T21:31:41Z

Codex: memory timeline returns to subagent threads after compaction

When Codex compacts a thread, the memory timeline drops out of its history. The root thread already got it back through SessionStart (source compact). Codex never sends SessionStart to subagent threads, though, so a subagent that compacted kept working without its memory.

  • A new PostCompact hook marks the compacted thread, keyed by session_id plus agent_id.
  • That thread's next PostToolUse records the observation as usual, consumes the marker, and returns the timeline once as additionalContext.
  • SessionStart clears the marker, so the root thread never gets the timeline twice.
  • Shared session-marker file handling now backs both the Codex compaction gate and the Kimi context gate.

PR: #4733

View release →

v13.38.0 Stable2026-10-13T18:36:36Z

No more console window flashing on Windows. Every Claude Code hook now calls claude-mem directly instead of going through bash, and this release also carries 16 community fixes from five contributors.

Hooks call claude-mem directly (#4345)

Every Claude Code runtime hook now runs a small claude-mem launcher in exec form ("command": "claude-mem", "args": [...]), with no shell in between. On Windows that ends the console window that used to flash and steal focus on every tool call (#3605, #3559, #3521, #3396, #3248).

  • npx claude-mem install / update compile the launcher on your machine. It goes to ~/.local/bin/claude-mem on macOS and Linux, or %LOCALAPPDATA%\claude-mem\bin\claude-mem.exe on Windows, and that folder is added to your PATH if it isn't there already. npx claude-mem uninstall removes the launcher.
  • Marketplace-only installs repair themselves. A startup step places or refreshes the launcher and tells you when Claude Code needs a restart.
  • The launcher fails open. On any problem it prints one line, claude-mem: <reason>, continuing without memory, and your session carries on.
  • The environment opt-outs (CLAUDE_MEM_DISABLE_TOOL_HOOKS, CLAUDE_MEM_DISABLE_OBSERVATION, CLAUDE_MEM_DISABLE_FILE_CONTEXT) still take effect before anything heavy starts.

Before you upgrade:

  • Hooks in exec form need Claude Code 2.1.139 or newer. install and doctor warn on older versions.
  • If the launcher's folder wasn't on your PATH yet, the first session after updating will tell you to restart Claude Code from a new terminal. After that, everything works as before.

Thanks @bagr001 for testing it on Windows 11.

Observer and worker

  • Gateways that reject the thinking parameter no longer stall the observer (#4735, @akushonkamen). The observer retries once without the parameter. The token-saving default is unchanged, and CLAUDE_MEM_OBSERVER_THINKING is now documented.
  • The worker no longer wedges for hours on big repos (#4753, @akushonkamen). Git commands during worktree adoption now run without blocking the event loop.
  • The streamed-request idle window is configurable (#4752, @akushonkamen) with CLAUDE_MEM_LLM_STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS, for self-hosted backends with long queue waits. The default is still 90 seconds.
  • CLAUDE_MEM_DISABLE_SUMMARY=1 skips the end-of-session summary for headless or read-only runs (#4736, @akushonkamen).
  • Mode prompts are validated, and a user mode file can no longer shadow a bundled mode (#4326).
  • Three platform fixes (#3851):
    • manual sessions keep their platform;
    • an error-shaped response is no longer taken for a host observer;
    • Grok agents added after install are watched.
  • The Codex installer drops a dead local marketplace registration, and install fails when Codex doesn't list the plugin afterwards (#3494).

Search, viewer and data, thank you @rudycelekli

  • Semantic search no longer breaks on empty filters (#4754) or on malformed stored concept or file data (#4750). It now matches keyword search.
  • The Sessions list matches project names regardless of case (#4749).
  • Feed rows with the same timestamp come back in a stable newest-first order (#4747).
  • Repeated prompts with their own native turn ids are no longer merged into one (#4748).
  • Restoring an export keeps each prompt's native turn id (#4751).
  • Deleting a project's last memories brings the welcome hint back right away (#4742).
  • A corpus built without a limit gets the documented 500 observations, and limits above 100 are no longer capped at 100 (#4746).

Sync and server

  • Chroma writes for the same memory are serialized and re-read the current row (#4731, @junnhwan). A startup backfill or a delayed cloud forward can no longer index an old body or old project.
  • On the self-hosted server, a reclaimed generation attempt can't complete or fail its replacement (#4739, @junnhwan). Postgres regression tests now run in CI.
  • The self-hosted sync server puts brackets around IPv6 hosts in its advertised URL (#4723, @DeryFerd).

Viewer

  • The NOTES section icon is sized like its siblings (#4744, @phant0um, first contribution). It used to render about 20× too large.

Claude app (cowork) plugin

  • Session-init now runs in the background, and the plugin's hooks stand down when a local claude-mem install is present (#4755).

Docs and skills

  • The make-plan skill has a Primary Goal section and a fixed short chat summary (#3600).
  • The wowerpoint skill stops once the deck has started and no longer uploads (#4342).
  • Docs no longer mention workers.dev for the sync hub (#4366).

Thanks to everyone who reported and fixed things this round: @rudycelekli, @junnhwan, @akushonkamen, @DeryFerd, @phant0um, @valerianbbragi, @smashingtags, @wuding129, @chris-yyau, @bagr001.

Full diff: https://github.com/thedotmack/claude-mem/compare/v13.37.0...v13.38.0

can1357/oh-my-pi ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v18.10.4 Stable2026-10-13T19:38:04Z

@oh-my-pi/pi-agent-core

Added

  • AgentTool.formatApprovalDetails receives ApprovalDetailsOptions; compact: true asks for untruncated bodies so a compact approval prompt can count their lines (#15550 by @H4vC)

Changed

  • Before-model-call hooks now receive the loop context as a second argument, and buildProviderContext receives the summarizer model (#15436 by @will-bogusz)

Fixed

  • Fixed a Claude compaction or branch summarizer receiving assistant reasoning from a snapcompact archive rendered under another model, which Claude refuses (reasoning_extraction) (#15436 by @will-bogusz)

@oh-my-pi/pi-ai

Fixed

  • Fixed Codex cybersecurity policy errors incorrectly retrying without Daybreak and disabling it for subsequent requests, while preserving retries without Daybreak when access is rejected (#15472 by @taibenvenuti)

Removed

  • Removed the toolNamespacesInfo stream option; Codex requests no longer send tool_namespaces_info turn metadata, and code_mode_tool_names/tool_namespaces_info are no longer reserved client metadata keys.

@oh-my-pi/pi-catalog

Added

  • Added Muse Code's subscription-only Muse Wonder Full and Wonder contributor tiers with the max effort, recording API-equivalent cost instead of $0: Wonder Full at Muse Wonder's $4 input / $0.20 cached / $20 output per 1M tokens, contributor tiers at the measured $1.60 / $0.08 / $8 (#15522 by @eggpeat)
  • Added the classifier-cot-extraction rule axis, set for Claude, marking models whose classifier refuses earlier reasoning quoted back into their input (#15436 by @will-bogusz)
  • Added Gemini 4 Argon pricing on Cursor ($2 input / $0.10 cached / $10 output per 1M tokens) and Google Antigravity, which now uses Google's first-party rate instead of showing $0 (#15620 by @eggpeat)
  • Added scheduled list-price changes to cost-patch, as cost-fallback already allowed: a dated effective-rates card sets the price from its date, and each request is priced at the card in force when it was made, even on models loaded before the change, on rebuilt models, and on Cursor lanes priced from a sibling's card. A malformed dated card now fails the model build instead of silently keeping the old price. getEffectiveTokenCost returns that card for price displays, and hasRecurringTariff tells these dated-only schedules apart from peak/off-peak tariffs, which alone show a status-line arrow (#15620 by @eggpeat)

Changed

  • Devin models with a long-context price tier (GPT-5.4 through 5.6 standard lanes, GPT-6 through 6.1, Gemini Pro, and Grok 4.5 through 4.7) now default to a 272K or 200K context window, where that pricing starts; run /extended-context on to use the full window at long-context rates (#14891 by @eggpeat).
  • Devin SWE-2, SWE-1.7, and GLM-5.2 fallback pricing no longer charges cache writes at the input rate, since Devin publishes no cache-write rate for them (#14891 by @eggpeat).
  • Google Antigravity now lists Gemini 4 Argon once, with selectable low, medium, and high thinking levels, instead of a separate entry per thinking tier (#15620 by @eggpeat)
  • Gemini 4 Argon on Google, Vertex, Google Antigravity, and Cursor moves to Google's $4 input / $0.20 cached / $20 output list price on 2026-12-01 (midnight US Pacific), including requests on models loaded before then, without a catalog update (#15620 by @eggpeat)
  • Devin SWE-2's promotional fallback price now ends on 2027-01-01 for requests on models loaded before then too (#15620 by @eggpeat)

Fixed

  • Fixed Devin Claude and GPT-5.6 through GPT-6 models pricing prompt-cache writes at $0; they now use Devin's published rate of 1.25x input, and Adaptive uses its input rate (#14891 by @eggpeat).
  • Fixed Devin GPT-5.4 through GPT-6.1, Gemini Pro, and Grok 4.5 through 4.7 models missing the long-context price tiers Devin publishes above 272K and 200K prompt tokens (#14891 by @eggpeat).
  • Fixed Devin Fusion pairings ignoring their lead model's rules, including its cache-write rate and long-context tier (#14891 by @eggpeat).
  • Fixed Kilo and NanoGPT models showing stale or missing prices (for example Gemini 4 Argon at $4/$20 instead of $2/$10, and many models at $0); prices now come from each gateway's live model list (#15623 by @eggpeat)
  • Fixed NanoGPT models showing their raw id as the name and no context window (disabling auto-compaction) when the bundled catalog lacked them; names, context windows and output limits now come from NanoGPT's live model list (#15623 by @eggpeat)

Removed

  • Removed the Codex toolMode model field (code_mode_only); Codex discovery no longer maps tool_mode

@oh-my-pi/pi-coding-agent

Added

  • Extensions can observe and answer the interactive plan review, including refinement feedback and save-and-quit (#14250 by @rlfleming93)
  • Added opt-in omp gc --compress (or gc.compress), which stores sessions idle for gc.compressAfterDays (default 7) with filesystem transparent compression (APFS, NTFS); they stay resumable and searchable (#15600 by @wolfiesch)
  • Extensions can reopen a dismissed plan review while the session is idle (ctx.openPlanReview()) and pick the execution tier on ctx.resolvePlanReview (input.role); plan_review_requested now reports the slider's tiers (#15552 by @Shadorain)

Changed

  • Tool approval prompts now replace the composer like ask, show one row per detail, and approve with Ctrl+Enter (or Ctrl+Q) instead of Enter (#15548 by @H4vC)

Fixed

  • Fixed late completion probes rewinding resumed conversations and stale history scans lowering parked subagent costs (#15594 by @anatoli-tsinovoy).
  • Fixed read.renderMarkdown having no effect on plain-file reads: inline read previews now render Markdown files as formatted Markdown instead of numbered source (#15007).
  • Fixed Claude refusing every turn (reasoning_extraction) in a session snapcompacted under Codex, Grok or another reasoning model, after a switch or resume; Claude turns, /btw, /handoff and compaction now read a reasoning-free copy of the archive (#15436 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed the project-shared browser (headless or headed) reopening every page its previous run left open whenever it relaunched (after a crash, an out-of-memory kill, a normal stop, or a restart from omp ps); no session owned those pages, so they were never closed and piled up across restarts. Detaching it through proc://<name>/mode, which relaunched it the same way, is now refused (#15561 by @eggpeat)
  • Fixed omp models --json reporting a scheduled model's superseded price in cost; it reports the price in force, such as DeepSeek V4 Pro's $0.30 / $1.20 card since 2026-09-14 (#15620 by @eggpeat)

Removed

  • Removed Codex Code Mode and its providers.openai-codex.codeMode / providers.openai-codex.codeModeDirectTools settings; Codex models always use the full direct tool surface.

@oh-my-pi/pi-natives

Fixed

  • Fixed pwsh code blocks rendering without PowerShell syntax colors (#15008).

@oh-my-pi/snapcompact

Added

  • Added viewWithoutThinking(), textWithoutThinking(), readerIncludesThinking() and an optional Archive.withoutThinking variant, so an archive rendered with reasoning can be read by a model that must not see it; without a stored variant the view keeps only reasoning-free text and no frames (#15436 by @will-bogusz)

Fixed

  • Fixed re-rendering an archive without reasoning keeping every paragraph after the first of a multi-paragraph ¶think: section (#15436 by @will-bogusz)

@oh-my-pi/omp-stats

Fixed

  • Usage recorded at $0 for a model whose catalog price changes on a date (Gemini 4 Argon, Devin SWE-2) is now priced at the rate in force on the request's date, instead of staying $0 like peak/off-peak tariff usage (#15620 by @eggpeat)

@oh-my-pi/pi-tui

  • Plan review overlay can take a remote answer: it shows the remotely chosen execution tier and locks further input like a terminal approval (#15552 by @Shadorain)

Fixed

  • The model browser shows the price a model is billed at now, not the one current when its row was loaded: DeepSeek V4 Pro shows its $0.30 / $1.20 card in effect since 2026-09-14 instead of $1.32 / $3.96, and Gemini 4 Argon switches to $4 / $20 on 2026-12-01 (#15620 by @eggpeat)
  • Clicking Copy answer in the /btw history (and Copy raw in the raw SSE viewer) on native terminals copies the answer instead of the button's label (#15547 by @H4vC)
  • Fixed image previews inside tmux showing as SIXEL IMAGE (WxH) placeholder text when the outer terminal lacks SIXEL; SIXEL is now enabled from tmux's DA1 reply only when tmux's terminal description for the attached client has Sxl (#15513)

Removed

JuliusBrussee/caveman ↗ 4 releases · 4 stable
v4.0.1Caveman 4.0.1 Stable2026-10-13T18:34:04Z

Caveman 4.0.1 fixes what turned up in the first hours of 4.0.0: a Windows uninstall bug, a sync that could never catch up, and problems on OpenCode 2 and Pi.

Windows

  • caveman off --all left the Caveman Blocks hook in Claude Code's settings, and every caveman setup added another copy. Blocks v0.1.0-rc.5 installs its binary as .exe, recognises the entries rc.4 wrote, and replaces them on the next caveman setup.
  • An npm .cmd shim that forwards to a native .exe launches again.
  • Hook child processes no longer flash a console window.

Sync

  • caveman sync uploads a large backlog of local spans in batches. Before, a store over the 16 MiB import cap failed with "Request body exceeds configured limit" on every retry.
  • The ExperimentalWarning: SQLite line is gone.
  • Known issue: the practice-findings part of caveman sync still reports "Request rejected". That is a Caveman Cloud bug and needs no CLI update once Cloud is fixed.

OpenCode

  • On OpenCode 2, a subagent spawn reset the mode for every session, undoing "stop caveman" and /ultracave. Subagent sessions no longer touch the mode.
  • The "provider is not routed" warning reads where a provider actually sends requests.

Pi

  • caveman wrap pi after caveman enable pi loaded the extension twice and Pi exited 1.
  • Routing a model no longer resets the thinking level (--thinking high stayed only until session start).
  • Works with Pi 1.1+ custom-message turns.
  • @caveman-ai/pi 0.3.1: the npm package now reads Caveman 4's config and port.

Claude Code and Codex

  • The prompt hook gives up after 2 seconds when the host sends no complete payload, like the other hooks.
  • Blocks honors CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR and CODEX_HOME; it used to write the hook into ~/.claude regardless.
  • caveman status no longer says "account refresh offline" after signing in to a different account.

Smaller fixes

  • Proxy: one key sent in both auth headers keeps its bearer; a Termux-built proxy is recognised as Caveman's own.
  • Memory: longer retry on SQLite contention.
  • /caveman-compress asks for a file path instead of improvising on pasted text, and says that the file goes to your model provider.
  • Tested agent pins moved to current Claude Code, Codex, OpenCode, Gemini, Kilo and Qwen releases.

Versions

  • @caveman-ai/cli 4.0.0 → 4.0.1, binaries bin-v4.0.0 → bin-v4.0.1, @caveman-ai/pi 0.3.0 → 0.3.1, Blocks v0.1.0-rc.4 → v0.1.0-rc.5.

Companion releases

  • Signed binaries, module index and container: bin-v4.0.1
  • npm CLI: @caveman-ai/cli@4.0.1
  • npm Pi extension: @caveman-ai/pi@0.3.1

View release →

v4.0.0Caveman 4.0.0 Stable2026-10-13T16:32:02Z

Caveman 4.0.0 makes npx @caveman-ai/cli the one install, adds Auto routing through Caveman Cloud on top of the local proxy, and ships Caveman Blocks as a module.

One command to install

  • npx @caveman-ai/cli opens a one-screen setup. It finds your coding agents and their logins (every Claude Code profile), shows what it will change, applies on Enter, then offers to start the agent. Without a terminal it applies only with --yes.
  • install.sh and install.ps1 end in the same setup, pinned to the CLI this release ships.
  • caveman on|off switches modules from a registry. The module index (modules.json) is listed in the signed checksum manifest of the binary release, and the CLI refuses an index that manifest does not list. A module lockfile records what is installed.
  • New commands: caveman stop, a status grid, an offline caveman doctor, caveman providers add|remove|login|local and caveman providers cloud on|off.
  • One config home: ~/.caveman/cloud.json. Older config migrates once, without races, and the telemetry opt-out carries over.
  • Sign-in uses the shared device flow. It refuses plain-HTTP Clouds other than this machine.

Auto routing (signed in to Caveman Cloud)

  • caveman-auto shows up in the Claude Code and OpenCode model pickers with a 1M context window. Routing applies only to Auto, and that includes Claude Pro/Max logins and ChatGPT logins (Codex, OpenCode).
  • For each routable request the proxy asks Cloud's route stage for a model and effort (contracts route-ask-v1) and picks from the providers you set up. If the key is refused or a billing limit is hit, the request goes through unrouted and caveman status says why.
  • Translators between Messages, Chat Completions and Responses cover all nine directions, so Claude Code can run on Responses hosts such as the ChatGPT plan.
  • A signed-in runtime sends runtime/v1 event batches to Cloud and respects the telemetry opt-out.

Cache

  • The proxy keeps the Anthropic prompt cache warm while a session is paused. Warm timing comes from measured return times, at most two warms run at once, and caveman stats lists every warm.
  • A session stays on its model across Cloud pauses and transient failures, so a model switch doesn't rewrite the cached prefix.

Caveman Blocks

  • The binary release mirrors caveman-ai/blocks v0.1.0-rc.4 (pinned by the digest of its checksum list). Each Blocks binary is republished under the same signed manifest as the other binaries. Blocks usage counts fall under the same telemetry notice and opt-out as Caveman's.

Catalog

  • Adds Sonnet 5.5, Haiku 5.5, GPT-6.1 Sol and GPT-6 Luna.

Versions

  • @caveman-ai/cli goes from 2.1.0 to 4.0.0 to match the product version. Binaries move from bin-v2.1.0 to bin-v4.0.0.
  • The npx github:JuliusBrussee/caveman installer depends on CLI ^4.0.0, so -- --uninstall runs a CLI that understands what 4.0 wrote.

Companion releases

  • Signed binaries, module index and container: bin-v4.0.0
  • npm CLI: @caveman-ai/cli@4.0.0

Full changes: https://github.com/JuliusBrussee/caveman/compare/v3.2.0...v4.0.0

Release smoke: https://github.com/JuliusBrussee/caveman/actions/runs/38431102855

View release →

CloakHQ/CloakBrowser ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
chromium-v154.0.8037.57.2-proChromium v154.0.8037.57.2 — Stealth Build (Pro) Stable2026-10-13T18:29:30Z

CloakBrowser Pro: Linux Stable advances to Chromium 154.0.8037.57.2

Chromium 154.0.8037.57.2 is now Stable on Linux (x64 and ARM64). Windows and macOS Stable stay on 154.0.8037.57.1.

With a valid CloakBrowser key, the wrapper automatically selects the Stable build for your platform. No version pin is required.

What's new since 154.0.8037.57.1 (Linux)

  • 3 to 6 times less CPU when running headed in Docker or on a GPU-less Linux server. Headless mode and machines with a real GPU are not affected.
  • Safer proxy handling. Proxy passwords with special characters now work, and a proxy setting the browser cannot read stops the connection instead of going out without the proxy.
  • A more patient license connection, with a new --license-route=auto|direct|proxy flag. auto is the default and works as before.
  • Windows persona hardware follows the graphics model. On Intel graphics profiles, a fixed seed may report different core and memory values than before.
  • Windows persona fonts match real Windows by default when the real Windows fonts are installed, so --fingerprint-windows-font-metrics is no longer needed.
  • The macOS persona on Linux matches a real Mac in two more details.

Your profiles, cookies, and seeds are untouched. Only the browser itself is replaced.

Current wrapper: v0.6.2

Update the wrapper before launching:

pip install -U cloakbrowser
npm update cloakbrowser
dotnet add package CloakBrowser --version 0.6.2

Roll back if needed

If a workload regresses, pin the previous Linux Stable build:

export CLOAKBROWSER_VERSION=154.0.8037.57.1

Remove the pin to return to the newest Stable build.

If you hit a regression, please tell us. Open an issue or email support@cloakbrowser.dev with the site and what you saw. Pinning the old build gets you unblocked; a report gets it fixed for everyone.

Checksums (SHA-256)

cee9eeda5d64b0a477b3a3f2ea6c99edb6fdd3ef840b91eca90ff4f37e538a63  cloakbrowser-linux-x64.tar.gz
4a648032b2a1f96fc30bc1dcb73c608abe1c09654e92d28844c50e50e915fb61  cloakbrowser-linux-arm64.tar.gz

The signed manifest (SHA256SUMS and SHA256SUMS.sig) is attached. The wrapper verifies its Ed25519 signature and each archive's SHA-256 checksum automatically.

tt-a1i/archify ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v3.1.0 Stable2026-10-13T17:23:05Z

Archify 3.1.0

https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/d6144aba-cb48-4692-a24d-b43bf7be62c2

30 seconds of Archify 3.1: five new diagram types, Live motion, light and dark.

Ten diagram types, with Live motion enabled by default: ERD, Class/interface, Tree, Timeline and Waterfall join Architecture, Workflow, Sequence, Dataflow and Lifecycle.

  • Type-specific motion stays separate from authored topology; switch to Still when needed.
  • Clearer cards, routing/label coordination, Sequence notes, Lifecycle layouts, and long-page reader navigation.
  • Structured authoring diagnostics, bounded recovery, source verification and distinct input/artifact/browser delivery gates.
  • Export, clipboard and Windows path portability fixes; a localized optional repository link inside the reader-opened Guide.

Full changelog · Release scope and trial-use evidence

Download archify.zip below, or use the canonical Skill source. Existing installations update only when their owner chooses to upgrade.

Known limitations include initial-viewport control overlap in some long Sequence diagrams, shared visual corridors in some Dataflow diagrams, and awkward wrapping of long Class generic types. Automated validation does not replace perceptual review. Timeline/Waterfall values require evidence to be described as factual/measured; illustrative examples remain labelled.

The independently published DSH plugin remains 1.0.0 / bundled Archify 3.0.1; its synchronization is a separate follow-up. Expanded public examples, README previews and website entry points are tracked in #916, #917 and #918.

code-yeongyu/oh-my-openagent ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v5.1.33 Stable2026-10-13T16:40:29Z

omo runs on senpi 2026.10.13: a busy host gives memory back when its sessions end. An RPC host serving many sessions used to keep the heap of every session it had run until it exited, so a long day of terminal-heavy work held its peak footprint; it now returns freed heap as sessions drop (senpi#3240, senpi#3221). Tool calls through DeepSeek's official API, and gateways that forward to it, stop failing with 400 Invalid schema ... got 'type: null', because every tool schema now declares a root type: "object" (senpi#3250). The Claude subscription lane runs Claude Code 2.1.296 (senpi#3258), the model catalog is refreshed from the live providers (senpi#3248), large eval data cells no longer stall the shared host (senpi#3206), and RPC clients see a scheduled goal continuation and can run it now (senpi#3208). Full notes: senpi 2026.10.13.

Daemon-hosted background tasks keep running when their parent exits or starts a new session. The parent detaches without aborting their turns. On /new or exit, one notice names the old session and the omo --resume command to collect results; children stay owned by that session. Local in-process and stdio children retain their shutdown behavior. (#8517, #10018)

A child that stops answering no longer hangs a task indefinitely. Stdio RPC requests have deadlines, and both process runners keep one heartbeat in flight. Three missed ten-second probes end a running child as child-unresponsive, preserve its transcript, and name how to inspect and rerun it in task_output. The last successful heartbeat appears as last_seen_at in the live task snapshot. (#10017)

Resuming a background task no longer waits for a free slot. When its concurrency group is full, resuming a finished background task now returns at once as pending and sends the follow-up when a slot frees, instead of blocking the call. A second resume of the same task while one is queued is refused, cancelling a queued resume releases nothing it didn't hold, and a fallback retry keeps the latest follow-up prompt. Thanks to @imadal1n. (#9985)

A task child started as its own process never receives its fallback chain after its first prompt. When the engine answered the capability check only after the chain deadline, the runner still sent set_retry_fallback once the first prompt was already out; it now drops the chain at the deadline, and the child keeps the manager's fallback with the usual "no answer" warning. This was the intermittent windows-latest test failure. (#9582, #10087)

ulw-execute stops a checkpoint that keeps failing instead of retrying it for days. After the same failure twice in a row, a third failed attempt, or four hours open, the checkpoint and every checkpoint that depends on it are marked blocked ([~]) with the gap left, recorded in the ledger and the final report, and the run moves on to the rest of the plan. A broken test harness gets one repair attempt, and work found while a checkpoint is open is queued after it unless it blocks that checkpoint. (#10051)

Mass research plans its whole collection before it starts instead of one wave at a time. mass ulw research now defines every angle as a harvest, expand and verify chain that passes its findings to the next step through a file, so a fast angle reaches verification while slow ones are still harvesting, and leads that are still open join each cluster of angles as soon as that cluster settles. The mass-ulw and ulw-execute skills no longer say a single node's completion wakes the session; only a finished or paused run does. (#10049)

herdrdev/herdr ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.9.4 Stable2026-10-13T15:41:22Z

Added

  • herdr plugin update updates plugins installed from GitHub, all at once or by name. Running plugin panes keep working on their old files, and new runs pick up the update without a config reload. Locally linked plugins are left alone. (#4245, thanks @JJLiebig)
  • Windows notifications stay in Notification Center. Clicking one jumps to its pane and brings the terminal window forward. (#4773, #4803, thanks @JJLiebig)

Changed

  • An elevated Windows server now only accepts administrator clients by default. To let normal clients from the same account in, set [server] allow_unelevated_clients = true or start the server with --allow-unelevated-clients. Permission errors now say how to fix them, and sessions you can't reach show as unavailable instead of stopped. (#4717, #4769, #4777, #4779, thanks @JJLiebig)
  • On Linux and macOS, closing the shell that started Herdr no longer stops the server. Scripts that stopped Herdr with SIGHUP should use herdr server stop or SIGTERM. The server log now says why the server stopped. (#4920, #4965)
  • With mouse capture on, plugins that handle a link get first pick when you click it. Turn capture off to use your terminal's own link shortcuts, like Command-click on macOS. (#4874, #5064, thanks @JJLiebig)
  • Antigravity and Grok count as idle once they are back at their prompt, even if their own background tasks or subagents are still running. (#3530, #4735, #4959)
  • Ctrl+Shift+letter reaches plain shells as the Ctrl key, like in Alacritty and WezTerm. Super, Hyper and Meta shortcuts no longer type escape-code junk into apps that can't read them. (#4356, #5017)

Fixed

  • Restarting a Mac or shutting down Windows keeps your agents' sessions. Herdr saves before the system starts closing apps, so agents come back where they were. (#4414, #5013, #5057, #5092, thanks @JJLiebig)
  • Session saves are written to disk before they replace the old file, so a crash or power cut can't leave an unreadable save. (#5150, #5152, thanks @JJLiebig)
  • The server keeps running when the machine runs out of processes or threads, instead of taking every pane down with it. (#3324, #4958)
  • An out-of-range workspace number returns workspace_not_found instead of crashing the server. (#5096, #5171)
  • Suspending an agent with Ctrl+Z and bringing it back with fg keeps its name and saved conversation. (#1647, #4964)
  • Restoring a session stays responsive when a checkout's Git data is slow or missing. Worktree groups survive checkouts that are temporarily unavailable, and opening the same repository twice no longer nests one workspace inside the other. (#4926, #4736, #4945, #4770, #4772, thanks @minatoaquaMK2 and @JJLiebig)
  • Closing a workspace's only pane asks first, like closing its last tab already does. Turn this off with ui.confirm_close = false. (#5143, #5173)
  • Ctrl shortcuts like Ctrl+W work on non-Latin keyboard layouts such as Russian, Greek or Hebrew. Latin layouts and AltGr characters keep their normal meaning. (#1079, #4961)
  • Accent keys and AltGr shortcuts work on Windows. (#4688, #5021, thanks @JJLiebig)
  • Shift+Enter and other enhanced keys keep working after the outer terminal resets, for example after VS Code's Reload Window. (#4985, #5088)
  • Quick Escape taps work on macOS with WezTerm's Kitty keyboard mode. (#1266, #5089)
  • Kitty keyboard apps get key releases and F1, F2 and F4 correctly, and Backspace works when the terminal sends Ctrl+H for it. (#4184, #4403, #3244, #5017)
  • Keyboard and terminal color replies no longer leak stray characters into pane input, and a half-finished color reply no longer freezes typing. (#4856, #4025, #5052, #5017, #5087)
  • Fish no longer repeats prompt fragments while you resize a pane. The same problem with zsh and Powerlevel10k is still open, waiting on a Ghostty fix. (#3672, #4977)
  • Apps that ask for pixel mouse coordinates get them, and clicks land in the right place on terminals that only report cells. (#4750, #3675, #5017, #4971)
  • Turning off Kitty graphics makes panes stop advertising image support, so apps fall back to text. (#4978, #4979)
  • Typing shows up faster, especially in PowerShell. (#4884, #5149, thanks @JJLiebig)
  • Big sessions use less CPU on busy agents and idle shells, hidden panes use less memory, and Windows panes need fewer threads. (#4506, #5083, #4845, #4847, #4849, #4850, #4873, #5085, #5091, thanks @JJLiebig)
  • New workspaces, tabs and splits start their shell at the right size instead of borrowing another pane's size. (#4419, #4873)
  • Forwarded SSH agents are no longer contacted every second, which could eventually break SSH and Git authentication. (#4931, #4962)
  • Sidebar width, collapsed groups and agent sorting on remote machines survive reconnects. Switching workspaces quickly while changing machines no longer gets canceled. (#4364, #5019, #4536, #5061, thanks @minatoaquaMK2 and @JJLiebig)
  • The sidebar collapse button stays clickable when the agent list has a scrollbar. (#4692, #4734, thanks @minatoaquaMK2)
  • The welcome screen shows your configured prefix. (#4745, #4747, thanks @JJLiebig)
  • Ctrl+[ leaves workspace navigation. (#1431, #2340, thanks @qaz74107410)
  • Keybindings that use + survive config reloads, and / opens Go To search on layouts where it needs Shift. (#4960, #4963, #4983, #5090, thanks @minatoaquaMK2)
  • Pasting from WezTerm on Windows into remote panes no longer types encoded key data. Copying no longer adds duplicate clipboard history entries, and closing a pane no longer hangs on a pending Enter. (#4725, #4730, #4709, #4872, thanks @JJLiebig)
  • Windows shells started through Scoop or 32-bit wrappers keep their working directory in new tabs and after restarts. (#4472, #4749, #5062, thanks @JJLiebig)
  • Windows ARM64 machines can be used as remote hosts through the x64 build, on a best-effort basis. (#4752)
  • Codex reports finished turns through its integration when started with --no-daemon, its startup update screen shows as blocked, and Codex and Pi status is recognized in more cases. (#4507, #4811, #4756, #5174, thanks @JJLiebig)
  • Antigravity permission, question and workspace-trust dialogs show as blocked, and its thinking spinner shows as working. (#3871, #3419, #3530, #4959)
  • Hermes started through its Python installer is recognized, and installing or removing its integration keeps your YAML plugin list intact. (#4910, #4909, #4911, #4984, thanks @minatoaquaMK2)
  • On macOS, pane run and agent start refuse commands too long for a shell that is still starting, instead of silently cutting them off. (#4950, #4988, thanks @minatoaquaMK2)

Removed

  • The experimental HERDR_WINDOWS_INPUT_BACKEND=crossterm setting is gone. Windows always uses its native console input. (#5017)

View release →

cline/cline ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
desktop-v0.0.46Desktop v0.0.46 Stable2026-10-13T15:03:59Z
  • A new outline along the left edge of the conversation lists each prompt you sent. Hover a mark to preview the prompt, and click it to jump there
  • Plugins that fail to load or error during a session now show a status line in the chat naming the plugin and the error, instead of silently losing their tools
  • You can cancel a Cline Cloud sign-in while it is waiting for the browser
  • Session titles renamed on the web now update in the app
  • The Cloud setup card stays visible on smaller windows when signed out
  • Installing a skill without Git installed now says Git is required and how to fix it, instead of showing a raw clone error
  • Scheduled routines: queued runs now start instead of staying pending forever, a run whose model call fails (for example, a retired model) is marked failed with the error, and after downtime only the latest missed occurrence runs instead of every one back to back. The routine editor now lists current models instead of retired ones
  • With no model chosen, the Cline provider uses the first model in Cline's recommended list (Claude Sonnet 5.5)
  • Turning reasoning off now works on every provider. Models that can't turn it off keep their default, and level-only Gemini models no longer reject the request with a thinking-budget error
  • Screenshots and other images no longer inflate the token estimate, which made auto-compaction run long before the conversation was near its limit
  • Auto-compaction uses the selected model's context window when the catalog entry has none
  • Rule files that are symlinks now load
  • Skills in .claude/skills are now discovered
  • The editor tool no longer receives null in place of the text to replace from some models
  • Refreshed the model catalog. Adds the AI/ML API, LowRouter, and Prime Inference providers. The v0 provider now offers only its default model, v0-1.5-md, and OpenCode Go uses its new https://opencode.ai/inference/go/v1 endpoint. The Cline free list adds Step 5 Preview. Default models change for 302.AI, CrossModel, Cortecs, DigitalOcean, NEAR AI Cloud, and Ofox (Claude Haiku 5.5), DevPass (LLM Gateway), LLM Gateway, and Meta (Muse Wonder 1.0), Aixy (GPT-6.1 Sol), CloudFerro Sherlock and Moark (GLM 5.3), Ambient, NanoGPT, Neuralwatt, and Novita AI

Full Changelog: https://github.com/cline/cline/compare/desktop-v0.0.45...desktop-v0.0.46

v4.1.24 Stable2026-10-13T15:02:27Z

Changed

  • With no model chosen, the Cline and OpenRouter providers now use Claude Sonnet 5.5. For Cline, that's the first model in Cline's recommended list.
  • Selecting ai& shows its own API key and model picker, instead of the generic OpenAI-compatible form with base URL and custom headers.
  • Refreshed the model catalog. Adds Claude Haiku 5.5 and the AI/ML API, LowRouter, and Prime Inference providers. The Cline free list adds Step 5 Preview. The v0 provider now offers only its default model, v0-1.5-md, and OpenCode Go uses its new https://opencode.ai/inference/go/v1 endpoint. Default models change for 302.AI, Cortecs, CrossModel, DevPass, DigitalOcean, Eden AI, GitHub Copilot, Google Vertex AI, LLM Gateway, Meta, Moark, NanoGPT, NEAR AI Cloud, Neuralwatt, Novita AI, Ofox, OpenCode Go, Requesty, Vivgrid, and a few others (most now Claude Haiku 5.5 or Muse Wonder 1.0). If you use one of those providers without pinning a model, expect a different default.

Fixed

  • Turning reasoning off no longer fails with a 400 error on GPT-6 Astra, GPT-6.1 Sol, Claude Fable 5 or Claude Opus 5.5, and now works on every provider. Level-only Gemini models no longer reject the request with a thinking-budget error.
  • An MCP server or hook that exits before reading all of its input no longer crashes the extension host about 3 seconds later.
  • A 404 from a stale or retired model id is no longer shown as a sign-in problem. Cline shows the real error instead of the logged-out card.
  • Errors in the Cline output log keep their details. Production builds used to drop them, so a line like "Cline OAuth login failed:" showed no reason.
  • Deleting a slash command from the input keeps the spacing of the text around it.
  • Screenshots and other images no longer inflate the token estimate, which made auto-compaction run long before the conversation was near its limit.
  • Auto-compaction uses the selected model's context window when the catalog entry has none.
  • Rule files that are symlinks now load, and skills in .claude/skills are now discovered.
  • The editor tool no longer receives null in place of the text to replace from some models.
  • Settings migrated from older versions no longer add an empty SAP AI Core provider.

Full Changelog: https://github.com/cline/cline/compare/v4.1.23...v4.1.24
